  1574	                creation = self._process_creation_time(process)
  1575	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1576	                if primary is None:
  1577	                    primary = error
  1578	        if primary is None and cleanup_error is None:
  1579	            # The creation is handed over to the caller: the original
  1580	            # handles are the property of the caller now and they leave
  1581	            # the holding table.  The handover is **one** decision of
  1582	            # the table: the process and the thread are reserved
  1583	            # together, and a handle that the recovery of the façade
  1584	            # claimed is never delivered and never forgotten here.
  1585	            reserved, refused = self._decide_entries(
  1586	                [(process, 'process'), (thread, 'thread')],
  1587	                self.TRANSFER_PENDING)
  1588	            if not refused:
  1589	                self._commit_entries(reserved)
  1590	                return CreatedProcess(process, thread, pid, creation, failures)
  1591	            # The recovery of the façade owns one of the original
  1592	            # handles: the creation may not deliver a stale handle to
  1593	            # the core.  The creation failed and the direct cleanup
  1594	            # below claims the entries that it may claim.
  1595	            self._restore_entries(reserved, self.API_OWNED)
  1596	            primary = BrokerError(
  1597	                'BROKER_PROCESS_HANDOVER_REFUSED',
  1598	                ','.join('%s:%s' % (kind, reason)
  1599	                         for _handle, kind, reason in refused))
  1600	        # The creation failed.  A process that was created is released
  1601	        # through its own original handle and every failure is named.
  1602	        release_failures = []
  1603	        if process is not None:
  1604	            self._release_created(process, thread, release_failures, cancellations)
  1605	        failures.extend(release_failures)
  1606	        if cancellations and primary is None:
  1607	            # The cancellation of the release is the primary error of the
  1608	            # creation when nothing else failed.  Every safe step was
  1609	            # executed before it is raised.
  1610	            primary = cancellations[0]
  1611	            failures.append('creation_cancelled:' + type(primary).__name__)
  1612	        error = primary if primary is not None else cleanup_error
  1613	        detail = getattr(error, 'detail', None)
  1614	        if failures:
  1615	            error.detail = ((detail or '') + '|cleanup=' + ','.join(failures))
  1616	        if cancellations and error is not cancellations[0]:
  1617	            # The primary error and the cancellation coexist: the
  1618	            # cancellation object stays reachable next to the primary
  1619	            # error and it is never replaced by a copy of a message.
  1620	            try:
  1621	                error.lvw_cancelled = cancellations[0]
  1622	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1623	                pass
  1624	        raise error
  1625	
  1626	    def _close_quietly(self, handle, failures, cancellations=None):
  1627	        """Close one handle and name a failure of the close.
  1628	
  1629	        A cancellation in the middle of a close never skips the close
  1630	        that follows it: it is named in the failures and it is handed to
  1631	        the caller, which raises it after every safe step was executed.
  1632	        """
  1633	        try:
  1634	            if not self._api.kernel32.CloseHandle(handle):
  1635	                failures.append('CloseHandle:last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1636	                return False
  1637	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  1638	            failures.append('CloseHandle:CANCELLED')
  1639	            if cancellations is not None:
  1640	                cancellations.append(error)
  1641	            return False
  1642	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1643	            failures.append('CloseHandle:' + type(error).__name__)
  1644	            return False
  1645	        return True
  1646	
  1647	    def _release_created(self, process, thread, failures, cancellations=None):
  1648	        """Release a process that was created but never adopted.
  1649	
  1650	        The process is stopped through its own original handle, its exit
  1651	        is confirmed and both of its handles are closed.  A failure of
  1652	        one of the steps is named and the other steps still run: a handle
  1653	        is never left to the garbage collector.  A cancellation in the
  1654	        middle of the release never skips the close that follows it: it
  1655	        is named and the release continues.
  1656	
  1657	        The direct cleanup of a failed creation is **one** decision of
  1658	        the table, exactly like the recovery of the façade and exactly
  1659	        like the handover of a pipe: the original entries are claimed
  1660	        before they are touched, the decision covers the process and
  1661	        the thread together and a handle that the recovery of the
  1662	        façade already claimed is **never** closed here.  The claim is
  1663	        taken under the lock of the table and the blocking primitives
  1664	        of the release run **outside** of it.
  1665	
  1666	        The confirmation of the exit and the confirmation of the close of
  1667	        a handle are two separate facts and they are recorded separately:
  1668	        a wait that timed out is not an exit and a close that failed is
  1669	        not a close.  A handle that could not be closed stays in the
  1670	        holding table of the façade: the original reference is kept and
  1671	        the recovery of the façade can reach it.
  1672	
  1673	        The **control handle of the process** is the one exception: it
  1674	        is closed only when the exit was really confirmed.  A process
  1675	        whose exit was not confirmed may still be alive - it may be a
  1676	        suspended process that never ran - and its control handle is the
  1677	        only handle that may stop it.  The handle stays in the holding
  1678	        table of the façade with its owner, so that the recovery of the
  1679	        façade can reach it again.
  1680	
  1681	        The cancellations of the release are collected in the list of
  1682	        the caller: they are raised after every safe step was executed
  1683	        and they never replace the primary error of the creation.
  1684	        """
  1685	        if cancellations is None:
  1686	            cancellations = []
  1687	        entries = [(process, 'process')]
  1688	        if thread is not None:
  1689	            entries.append((thread, 'thread'))
  1690	        claimed, refused = self._decide_entries(entries, self.RECOVERY_CLAIMED)
  1691	        for _handle, kind, reason in refused:
  1692	            failures.append('create_release_refused:%s:%s' % (kind, reason))
  1693	        owned = {id(handle) for handle, _kind in claimed}
  1694	        closed = set()
  1695	        if id(process) in owned:
  1696	            try:
  1697	                if not self._api.kernel32.TerminateProcess(process, 1):
  1698	                    failures.append('TerminateProcess:last_error=%d'
  1699	                                    % self._last_error())
  1700	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  1701	                failures.append('TerminateProcess:CANCELLED')
  1702	                cancellations.append(error)
  1703	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1704	                failures.append('TerminateProcess:' + type(error).__name__)
  1705	            confirmed = self._confirm_process_exit(process, failures, cancellations)
  1706	            if not confirmed:
  1707	                # The process may still be alive.  Its control handle
  1708	                # stays with the façade: it is never closed and never
  1709	                # forgotten.
  1710	                failures.append('process_control_handle_retained:exit_not_confirmed')
  1711	                self._note(process, 'exit_not_confirmed:control_handle_retained')
  1712	            elif self._close_quietly(process, failures, cancellations):
  1713	                self._forget(process)
  1714	                closed.add(id(process))
  1715	            else:
  1716	                self._note(process, 'close_not_confirmed')
  1717	        # The thread handle is independent of the exit of the process: it
  1718	        # is closed on its own and it never waits for the process.
  1719	        if thread is not None and id(thread) in owned:
  1720	            if self._close_quietly(thread, failures, cancellations):
  1721	                self._forget(thread)
  1722	                closed.add(id(thread))
  1723	            else:
  1724	                self._note(thread, 'close_not_confirmed')
  1725	        # A claim that did not end with a confirmed close is given back:
  1726	        # a later recovery of the round may claim the entry again and it
  1727	        # may confirm the exit.
  1728	        for handle, _kind in claimed:
  1729	            if id(handle) not in closed:
  1730	                self._release_claim(id(handle))
  1731	        if cancellations:
  1732	            failures.append('release_cancelled:' + type(cancellations[0]).__name__)
  1733	        return failures
  1734	
  1735	    def _process_creation_time(self, process):
  1736	        """The birth time of one process, as the 100 nanosecond tick count.
  1737	
  1738	        The query is the identity of the process: a process whose birth
  1739	        time cannot be read has no proven identity and is not used.
  1740	        """
  1741	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1742	        creation = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
  1743	        exit_time = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
  1744	        kernel = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
  1745	        user = self._api.types['FILETIME']()
  1746	        if not self._api.kernel32.GetProcessTimes(
  1747	                process, ctypes.byref(creation), ctypes.byref(exit_time),
  1748	                ctypes.byref(kernel), ctypes.byref(user)):
  1749	            error = self._last_error()
  1750	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PROCESS_BIRTH_QUERY_FAILED',
  1751	                              'last_error=%d' % error)
  1752	        return (int(creation.dwHighDateTime) << 32) | int(creation.dwLowDateTime)
  1753	
  1754	    def assign_process_to_job(self, job, process):
  1755	        if not self._api.kernel32.AssignProcessToJobObject(job, process):
  1756	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_ASSIGN_FAILED',
  1757	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1758	        return True
  1759	
  1760	    def resume_thread(self, thread):
  1761	        result = self._api.kernel32.ResumeThread(thread)
  1762	        if result == 0xFFFFFFFF:
  1763	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_RESUME_FAILED',
  1764	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1765	        return int(result)
  1766	
  1767	    def terminate_process(self, process, exit_code):
  1768	        if not self._api.kernel32.TerminateProcess(process, exit_code):
  1769	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_TERMINATE_FAILED',
  1770	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1771	        return True
  1772	
  1773	    def wait_process(self, process, timeout_ms):
  1774	        result = self._api.kernel32.WaitForSingleObject(process, timeout_ms)
  1775	        if result == self.WAIT_OBJECT_0:
  1776	            return True
  1777	        if result == self.WAIT_TIMEOUT:
  1778	            return False
  1779	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_WAIT_FAILED', 'wait=%d' % int(result))
  1780	
  1781	    def exit_code(self, process):
  1782	        code = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
  1783	        if not self._api.kernel32.GetExitCodeProcess(
  1784	                process, self._api.types['ctypes'].byref(code)):
  1785	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_EXIT_CODE_FAILED',
  1786	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1787	        return int(code.value)
  1788	
  1789	    def close_handle(self, handle):
  1790	        if not self._api.kernel32.CloseHandle(handle):
  1791	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED',
  1792	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1793	        # The close was confirmed: the handle leaves the holding table
  1794	        # of the façade.  A handle that could not be closed stays in it
  1795	        # with its owner and it is reachable by the recovery.
  1796	        self._forget(handle)
  1797	        return True
  1798	
  1799	    # -- the pipes ---------------------------------------------------------
  1800	    def create_pipe(self, name):
  1801	        """Create one pipe.  Only the write end is inheritable.
  1802	
  1803	        The security attributes of the pipe say that the ends are
  1804	        inheritable and the inherit flag of the *read* end is cleared
  1805	        right afterwards.  A pipe that was created with ``NULL``
  1806	        security attributes can never be inherited at all.
  1807	
  1808	        Both ends enter the holding table of this façade in the moment
  1809	        of their creation.  A pipe whose inherit flag could not be
  1810	        cleared therefore keeps its original ends with their owner: the
  1811	        core never received the pair and the round never loses the
  1812	        ownership of a handle that it created.
  1813	
  1814	        The failure of the inherit flag is cleaned up **independently**
  1815	        of the two ends.  A cancellation of the inherit flag and a
  1816	        cancellation of the close of one end never skip the close of the
  1817	        other end: the safe close of both ends always runs and the
  1818	        cancellation is raised afterwards.  The end that could not be
  1819	        closed keeps its original reference with the façade and the end
  1820	        that was closed with a confirmed close leaves the table.
  1821	        """
  1822	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1823	        read = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
  1824	        write = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
  1825	        attributes = self._security_attributes(True)
  1826	        if not self._api.kernel32.CreatePipe(ctypes.byref(read), ctypes.byref(write),
  1827	                                             ctypes.byref(attributes), 0):
  1828	            error = self._last_error()
  1829	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_FAILED', 'last_error=%d' % error)
  1830	        # The moment of the creation of the two ends.
  1831	        self._retain(read, 'pipe_read')
  1832	        self._retain(write, 'pipe_write')
  1833	        primary = None
  1834	        inherited = False
  1835	        try:
  1836	            inherited = bool(self._api.kernel32.SetHandleInformation(
  1837	                read, self.HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0))
  1838	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  1839	            # The cancellation of the inherit flag is the primary error
  1840	            # of the pipe and it never skips the safe close of the ends.
  1841	            primary = error
  1842	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1843	            primary = BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_INHERIT_FAILED',
  1844	                                  'last_error=%d:%s' % (self._last_error(),
  1845	                                                        type(error).__name__))
  1846	        if inherited:
  1847	            return read, write
  1848	        error = self._last_error()
  1849	        failures = []
  1850	        cancellations = []
  1851	        if self._close_quietly(read, failures, cancellations):
  1852	            self._forget(read)
  1853	        if self._close_quietly(write, failures, cancellations):
  1854	            self._forget(write)
  1855	        if primary is None:
  1856	            primary = BrokerError(
  1857	                'BROKER_PIPE_INHERIT_FAILED',
  1858	                'last_error=%d%s' % (error,
  1859	                                     ('|cleanup=' + ','.join(failures))
  1860	                                     if failures else ''))
  1861	        else:
  1862	            try:
  1863	                primary.detail = ((getattr(primary, 'detail', None) or '')
  1864	                                  + '|cleanup=' + ','.join(failures))
  1865	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1866	                pass
  1867	        if cancellations:
  1868	            # The cancellation of a close is the error that leaves the
  1869	            # method and the primary error stays reachable next to it:
  1870	            # a cancellation never becomes an ordinary evidence and it
  1871	            # never hides the error that caused the pipe.
  1872	            cancel = cancellations[0]
  1873	            try:
  1874	                cancel.lvw_primary = primary
  1875	                primary.lvw_cancelled = cancel
  1876	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  1877	                pass
  1878	            raise cancel
  1879	        raise primary
  1880	
  1881	    def read_stream(self, handle, size):
  1882	        """Read one block of a child stream.
  1883	
  1884	        The end of a stream is an end and not an error, but only in the
  1885	        branches that Windows documents as the end of a pipe: a broken
  1886	        pipe, a pipe that is not connected any more, the end of the file
  1887	        and an operation that was aborted.  Every other failure of the
  1888	        read is a failure of the round and it is named.
  1889	        """
  1890	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1891	        buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(size)
  1892	        read = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
  1893	        if not self._api.kernel32.ReadFile(handle, buffer, size,
  1894	                                           ctypes.byref(read), None):
  1895	            error = self._last_error()
  1896	            if error in (self.ERROR_BROKEN_PIPE, self.ERROR_PIPE_NOT_CONNECTED,
  1897	                         self.ERROR_HANDLE_EOF, self.ERROR_OPERATION_ABORTED):
  1898	                return b''
  1899	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_READ_FAILED', 'last_error=%d' % error)
  1900	        return buffer.raw[:read.value]
  1901	
  1902	    def write_stream(self, handle, data):
  1903	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1904	        buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(bytes(data), len(data))
  1905	        written = self._api.types['DWORD'](0)
  1906	        if not self._api.kernel32.WriteFile(handle, buffer, len(data),
  1907	                                            ctypes.byref(written), None):
  1908	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_WRITE_FAILED',
  1909	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1910	        return int(written.value)
  1911	
  1912	    # -- the cancellation of a synchronous operation -----------------------
  1913	    def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
  1914	        """The original handle of the calling thread.
  1915	
  1916	        The handle is the handle of the thread itself and never a self
  1917	        reported identifier: ``CancelSynchronousIo`` cancels the
  1918	        operation of one thread and it needs the real handle of that
  1919	        thread.
  1920	        """
  1921	        ctypes = self._api.types['ctypes']
  1922	        thread = self._api.kernel32.GetCurrentThread()
  1923	        process = self._api.kernel32.GetCurrentProcess()
  1924	        duplicate = self._api.types['HANDLE']()
  1925	        if not self._api.kernel32.DuplicateHandle(process, thread, process,
  1926	                                                  ctypes.byref(duplicate), 0,
  1927	                                                  False, self.DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS):
  1928	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_THREAD_HANDLE_FAILED',
  1929	                              'last_error=%d' % self._last_error())
  1930	        return duplicate
  1931	
  1932	    def cancel_synchronous_io(self, thread_handle):
  1933	        """Ask for the cancellation of the synchronous operation of a thread.
  1934	
  1935	        The call is a **request** and not a completion.  It may report
  1936	        ``ERROR_NOT_FOUND`` because the operation had not started yet or
  1937	        because it had just finished: the caller has to read the real
  1938	        completion of the thread and to join it.  The return value of
  1939	        this method names the state of the request.
  1940	        """
  1941	        if not self._api.kernel32.CancelSynchronousIo(thread_handle):
  1942	            error = self._last_error()
  1943	            if error == self.ERROR_NOT_FOUND:
  1944	                return 'NOT_FOUND'
  1945	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_FAILED',
  1946	                              'last_error=%d' % error)
  1947	        return 'REQUESTED'
  1948	
  1949	
  1950	# -- the fixed command of a role ------------------------------------------
  1951	
  1952	def _base_environment():
  1953	    environment = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items()
  1954	                   if key.upper() not in REMOVED_ENVIRONMENT}
  1955	    environment['PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE'] = '1'
  1956	    environment['PYTHONNOUSERSITE'] = '1'
  1957	    environment['NO_PROXY'] = '127.0.0.1,localhost'
  1958	    return environment
  1959	
  1960	
  1961	def _comfy_environment(root):
  1962	    environment = _base_environment()
  1963	    temporary = str(Path(root) / 'temp' / 'service-os')
  1964	    environment.update({
  1965	        'HF_HUB_OFFLINE': '1',
  1966	        'TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE': '1',
  1967	        'HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE': '1',
  1968	        'TEMP': temporary,
  1969	        'TMP': temporary,
  1970	        'TORCH_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'torch'),
  1971	        'HF_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'huggingface'),
  1972	        'XDG_CACHE_HOME': str(Path(temporary) / 'cache'),
  1973	    })
  1974	    return environment
  1975	
  1976	
  1977	def _ollama_environment():
  1978	    environment = _base_environment()
  1979	    environment['OLLAMA_HOST'] = '127.0.0.1:11434'
  1980	    environment['OLLAMA_NO_CLOUD'] = '1'
  1981	    return environment
  1982	
  1983	
  1984	def role_command(role, run_root, owned_pids):
  1985	    """Assemble the fixed command of one role.
  1986	
  1987	    The command form of a role is frozen in this module and it is
  1988	    assembled from the tools that the plan closed.  The plan never carries
  1989	    an argument vector, a shell, a script or an environment: it carries
  1990	    the tools and the closed data of the role, and this function is the
  1991	    only place where a command line of this package exists.
  1992	
  1993	    The forms are the ones of the already authorised launch of this pack:
  1994	    the ComfyUI launch of ``reports/v0-s1/wp-s1-03-phase-a/live_probe.py``
  1995	    and of ``tools/v0_s2_driver.py``, the Ollama launch of the same
  1996	    wrapper, and the sampler launch of ``tools/v0_s2_runtime.py``.
  1997	
  1998	    The private process ids of the sampler are passed as *separate*
  1999	    arguments: the parser of the sampler is ``type=int, nargs='+'`` and
  2000	    one argument that carries a space would not be an integer.
  2001	    """
  2002	    name = role['role']
  2003	    tools = role['tools']
  2004	    data = role['data']
  2005	    root = str(run_root)
  2006	    if name == 'COMFY':
  2007	        python = tools[0]['path']
  2008	        main = tools[1]['path']
  2009	        arguments = ['-s', '-B', main,
  2010	                     '--listen', '127.0.0.1', '--port', '8191',
  2011	                     '--base-directory', root,
  2012	                     '--user-directory', str(Path(root) / data['user_directory']),
  2013	                     '--disable-auto-launch', '--disable-api-nodes',
  2014	                     '--disable-all-custom-nodes',
  2015	                     '--whitelist-custom-nodes', 'lvw_v0_bridge']
  2016	        return {'executable': python, 'arguments': arguments,
  2017	                'working_directory': root, 'environment': _comfy_environment(root)}
  2018	    if name == 'OLLAMA':
  2019	        executable = tools[0]['path']
  2020	        return {'executable': executable, 'arguments': ['serve'],
  2021	                'working_directory': root, 'environment': _ollama_environment()}
  2022	    if name == 'SAMPLER':
  2023	        python = tools[0]['path']
  2024	        script = tools[1]['path']
  2025	        directory = str(Path(root) / 'temp' / 'resource-sampler')
  2026	        arguments = ['-I', '-B', script, '--execute-sampler',
  2027	                     '--run-root', directory,
  2028	                     '--nvidia-smi', data['nvidia_exe'],
  2029	                     '--binary-sha256', data['nvidia_sha256'],
  2030	                     '--private-pids'] + [str(pid) for pid in owned_pids] + \
  2031	                    ['--max-seconds', str(data['max_seconds'])]
  2032	        return {'executable': python, 'arguments': arguments,
  2033	                'working_directory': directory, 'environment': _base_environment()}
  2034	    raise BrokerError('BROKER_ROLE_UNKNOWN', name)
  2035	
  2036	
  2037	# -- the bounded reader of a child stream ----------------------------------
  2038	
  2039	class _BoundedStreamReader(threading.Thread):
  2040	    """One bounded background reader of one stream of a child.
  2041	
  2042	    The thread only reads.  It never writes, it never calls the broker and
  2043	    it never waits for the broker.  The broker takes the published counts
  2044	    out of it at its own checkpoints, so the deadline of the round is never
  2045	    held up by a silent child.
  2046	
  2047	    A read that failed for a reason that Windows does not document as the
  2048	    end of a pipe is a named error of the round: the reader keeps it and
  2049	    the round is not a release.
  2050	    """
  2051	
  2052	    def __init__(self, win32, handle, *, limit, name):
  2053	        super().__init__(daemon=True, name='lvw-native-custody-stream-' + name)
  2054	        self.win32 = win32
  2055	        self.handle = handle
  2056	        self.limit = limit
  2057	        self.observed = 0
  2058	        self.kept = 0
  2059	        self.overflow = False
  2060	        self.error = None
  2061	        self.finished = threading.Event()
  2062	
  2063	    def run(self):
  2064	        try:
  2065	            while True:
  2066	                block = self.win32.read_stream(self.handle, 65536)
  2067	                if not block:
  2068	                    break
  2069	                self.observed += len(block)
  2070	                if self.observed > self.limit:
  2071	                    self.overflow = True
  2072	                    break
  2073	                self.kept += len(block)
  2074	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2075	            self.error = getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__
  2076	        finally:
  2077	            self.finished.set()
  2078	
  2079	
  2080	# -- the pending resources of one round ------------------------------------
  2081	
  2082	def _json_fact(value):
  2083	    """One fact of the evidence of the round that is always serialisable.
  2084	
  2085	    A handle is an integer in the offline validation and an opaque
  2086	    object in a real run: the evidence of the round carries a printable
  2087	    name of it and it never carries the object itself.  A callable is
  2088	    never serialisable either and it is named instead.
  2089	    """
  2090	    if value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, int, float, str)):
  2091	        return value
  2092	    return repr(value)
  2093	
  2094	
  2095	class _PendingResources:
  2096	    """The pending resource table of one round.
  2097	
  2098	    Every handle that this round creates enters this table in the moment
  2099	    of its successful creation.  A handle **stays** in the table until it
  2100	    is closed with a confirmed close: the handover of a handle to its
  2101	    owner changes the owner and nothing else.  The handle is a resource
  2102	    that this round owns and it is named until it is really closed.
  2103	
  2104	    A handle that could not be closed stays in the table with its owner:
  2105	    it is a resource that this round still owns and it is named in the
  2106	    evidence of the round.
  2107	
  2108	    A handle of another owner is never in this table and it is never
  2109	    touched.
  2110	
  2111	    The table carries the **owner object** of a handle and not only its
  2112	    name: a handle that is owned by a live reader is protected from the
  2113	    pending pass by the fact that the reader is alive and not by a
  2114	    string that somebody may write.  The owner object is the reader
  2115	    itself and the table asks it.
  2116	    """
  2117	
  2118	    def __init__(self, win32):
  2119	        self._win32 = win32
  2120	        self._items = []
  2121	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
  2122	
  2123	    def add(self, handle, kind, *, role=None, owner=None, owner_ref=None,
  2124	            closeable=None):
  2125	        """Enter one handle into the table of the round.
  2126	
  2127	        The ``closeable`` predicate is the **close eligibility** of the
  2128	        handle.  It is asked by every close of the table and it is the
  2129	        authority of the question "may this handle be closed now?": a
  2130	        control handle of a process whose exit was not confirmed is
  2131	        never closed, and the predicate is the **owner** of that fact
  2132	        and not a name of it.
  2133	
  2134	        The ``owner_ref`` is the object of the owner of the handle: the
  2135	        pending pass asks it before it closes the handle.
  2136	        """
  2137	        item = {'handle': handle, 'kind': kind, 'role': role,
  2138	                'owner': owner or 'PENDING', 'owner_ref': owner_ref,
  2139	                'closeable': closeable, 'closed': False}
  2140	        with self._lock:
  2141	            self._items.append(item)
  2142	        return item
  2143	
  2144	    def take(self, handle):
  2145	        """Remove one handle from the table: its owner holds it now."""
  2146	        with self._lock:
  2147	            for index, item in enumerate(self._items):
  2148	                if item['handle'] == handle:
  2149	                    del self._items[index]
  2150	                    return item
  2151	        return None
  2152	
  2153	    def find(self, handle):
  2154	        """The entry of one handle, without removing it from the table.
  2155	
  2156	        The entry carries the **owner** of the handle and its close
  2157	        eligibility.  A close that is confirmed needs the owner of the
  2158	        handle in order to notify it: a notification that names a copy
  2159	        of the entry would notify nobody.
  2160	        """
  2161	        with self._lock:
  2162	            for item in self._items:
  2163	                if item['handle'] == handle:
  2164	                    return item
  2165	        return None
  2166	
  2167	    def transfer(self, handle, owner, *, owner_ref=None):
  2168	        """Hand one pending handle over to its owner.
  2169	
  2170	        The owner of the handle is only changed after the handover
  2171	        succeeded.  The handle stays in the table until it is closed: it
  2172	        is a resource that this round owns and it is named until then.
  2173	        A handle that could not be handed over keeps the owner
  2174	        ``PENDING`` and it is closed by the round.
  2175	
  2176	        The ``owner_ref`` is the **object** of the new owner.  It is
  2177	        what the pending pass asks before it closes a handle: a read end
  2178	        that a live reader still reads from is never closed under it.
  2179	        """
  2180	        with self._lock:
  2181	            for item in self._items:
  2182	                if item['handle'] == handle:
  2183	                    item['owner'] = owner
  2184	                    if owner_ref is not None:
  2185	                        item['owner_ref'] = owner_ref
  2186	                    return item
  2187	        return None
  2188	
  2189	    def pending(self):
  2190	        """The handles that this round still owns and has not closed."""
  2191	        with self._lock:
  2192	            return [dict(item) for item in self._items]
  2193	
  2194	    def pending_facts(self):
  2195	        """The pending handles of the round as **serialisable** facts.
  2196	
  2197	        The close eligibility of a handle is a **callable** over the
  2198	        owner of the handle and a callable is never serialised.  The
  2199	        facts name the eligibility as a name and they keep the
  2200	        **original** reference of the handle: a resource of the round is
  2201	        never replaced by a copy of a message.
  2202	        """
  2203	        facts = []
  2204	        for item in self.pending():
  2205	            predicate = item.get('closeable')
  2206	            if predicate is None:
  2207	                eligibility = 'NONE'
  2208	            elif callable(predicate):
  2209	                eligibility = 'CALLABLE'
  2210	            else:
  2211	                eligibility = 'FROZEN'
  2212	            facts.append({'handle': _json_fact(item.get('handle')),
  2213	                          'kind': item.get('kind'),
  2214	                          'role': item.get('role'),
  2215	                          'owner': item.get('owner'),
  2216	                          'closed': bool(item.get('closed')),
  2217	                          'eligibility': eligibility})
  2218	        return facts
  2219	
  2220	    def close_all(self, failures, on_closed=None, protect=None):
  2221	        """Close every handle that is still pending and name the failures.
  2222	
  2223	        A handle that could not be closed stays in the table: it is a
  2224	        resource that this round still owns and it is named in the
  2225	        evidence of the round.  It is never left to the garbage
  2226	        collector and it is never closed twice.
  2227	
  2228	        Every handle is closed on its own: a cancellation in the middle
  2229	        of the group never skips the handles that follow it.  The
  2230	        cancellation is raised after every handle was coordinated and it
  2231	        is never turned into an ordinary evidence.
  2232	
  2233	        A handle that was closed with a **confirmed** close is reported
  2234	        to the owner of the table through ``on_closed``: the core has to
  2235	        learn that the handle is really closed, so that it never names a
  2236	        closed handle any more.  The report happens before the
  2237	        cancellation is raised: the bookkeeping of the core is never
  2238	        left behind the error that left the cleanup.
  2239	
  2240	        The ``protect`` predicate is the **real** guard of the pass: a
  2241	        handle whose owner is still using it is never closed under it,
  2242	        whatever the branch that reached the pass did before.  The
  2243	        guard is asked about the owner and not about a name of an owner.
  2244	
  2245	        The **close eligibility** of a handle is asked here as well: a
  2246	        handle that may not be closed yet - the control handle of a
  2247	        process whose exit was not confirmed - is never closed by the
  2248	        pass.  The eligibility comes from the **same** owner that the
  2249	        release of the records asks: the pass may not decide the
  2250	        question on its own and it may not close behind the back of the
  2251	        owner of the handle.
  2252	        """
  2253	        remaining = []
  2254	        cancellations = []
  2255	        with self._lock:
  2256	            items = list(self._items)
  2257	        for item in items:
  2258	            if protect is not None and protect(item):
  2259	                # The handle is in use by a live owner: it is never
  2260	                # closed under it and it keeps its owner.
  2261	                remaining.append(item)
  2262	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2263	                                 'role': item['role'],
  2264	                                 'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_HANDLE_IN_USE'})
  2265	                continue
  2266	            if not self._is_closeable(item):
  2267	                # The handle may not be closed yet: it keeps its owner
  2268	                # and the round keeps the fact.
  2269	                remaining.append(item)
  2270	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2271	                                 'role': item['role'],
  2272	                                 'code': 'BROKER_PENDING_CLOSE_NOT_ELIGIBLE'})
  2273	                continue
  2274	            try:
  2275	                self._win32.close_handle(item['handle'])
  2276	            except BrokerError as error:
  2277	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2278	                                 'role': item['role'], 'code': error.code})
  2279	                remaining.append(item)
  2280	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  2281	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2282	                                 'role': item['role'],
  2283	                                 'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  2284	                                 'cancellation': True})
  2285	                remaining.append(item)
  2286	                cancellations.append(error)
  2287	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2288	                failures.append({'stage': 'close_pending', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2289	                                 'role': item['role'],
  2290	                                 'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  2291	                remaining.append(item)
  2292	            else:
  2293	                item['closed'] = True
  2294	                if on_closed is not None:
  2295	                    on_closed(item)
  2296	        with self._lock:
  2297	            self._items = remaining
  2298	        if cancellations:
  2299	            raise cancellations[0]
  2300	        return remaining
  2301	
  2302	    @staticmethod
  2303	    def _is_closeable(item):
  2304	        """Whether one pending handle may be closed now.
  2305	
  2306	        The predicate of the entry is the authority of the question.  A
  2307	        predicate that cannot answer protects the handle: an unknown
  2308	        fact is never a permission to close.
  2309	
  2310	        The predicate is a **callable** over the original owner of the
  2311	        handle.  A frozen answer - a boolean that was taken at the
  2312	        moment of the creation of the handle - is **not** an
  2313	        eligibility: it protects the handle, because the current fact
  2314	        of the owner is the only authority of the question.
  2315	        """
  2316	        predicate = item.get('closeable')
  2317	        if predicate is None:
  2318	            return True
  2319	        if not callable(predicate):
  2320	            # A frozen answer is not an eligibility.
  2321	            return False
  2322	        try:
  2323	            return bool(predicate())
  2324	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2325	            return False
  2326	
  2327	
  2328	# -- the core of the broker ------------------------------------------------
  2329	
  2330	class BrokerCore:
  2331	    """The job and the handle custody of one native custody round.
  2332	
  2333	    The core owns exactly one job object and the original handles of the
  2334	    processes of the round.  Every process of the round is created
  2335	    suspended, it is assigned to the job of the round and only then it is
  2336	    resumed.
  2337	
  2338	    A failure never falls back to a weaker behaviour.  There is no retry
  2339	    without the job, there is no adoption of a process that was not
  2340	    created here and there is no stop of a process that this core does not
  2341	    hold a handle of.
  2342	    """
  2343	
  2344	    def __init__(self, *, win32, plan, run_root, clock=None, wall=None,
  2345	                 context=None):
  2346	        self.win32 = win32
  2347	        self.plan = plan
  2348	        self.run_root = Path(run_root)
  2349	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  2350	        self._wall = wall or time.time
  2351	        # The ONE context of the round.  The core, the loop, the
  2352	        # transport of the channel and the endpoints of the channel hold
  2353	        # this very object: a layer that only copied the number of a
  2354	        # deadline would be a layer with a deadline of its own.
  2355	        self.context = context if context is not None else _RoundContext(
  2356	            clock=self._clock, wall=self._wall)
  2357	        # The façade of the primitives reads the absolute deadline and
  2358	        # the published cleanup deadline of the round from the **same**
  2359	        # context: a recovery of the façade may not open a budget of
  2360	        # its own.
  2361	        binder = getattr(self.win32, 'bind_context', None)
  2362	        if callable(binder):
  2363	            try:
  2364	                binder(self.context)
  2365	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2366	                pass
  2367	        self.state = 'NEW'
  2368	        self.job = None
  2369	        self.job_kill_on_close = False
  2370	        self.job_closed = False
  2371	        self.job_terminated = False
  2372	        self.job_active = None
  2373	        self.job_active_known = False
  2374	        self.job_process_ids = []
  2375	        self.records = []
  2376	        self.failures = []
  2377	        self.readers = []
  2378	        self.reader_states = []
  2379	        self.new_text_bytes = 0
  2380	        self.stop_reason = None
  2381	        self._stream_failure_reported = False
  2382	        self._text_failure_reported = False
  2383	        self._pending = _PendingResources(win32)
  2384	        # The close eligibility of the control handle of every record of
  2385	        # the round.  The map is keyed by the identity of the record and
  2386	        # it holds the **callable** over that record: the pending table
  2387	        # and the release of the records ask the very same callable and
  2388	        # they never disagree about the current exit fact.
  2389	        self._process_eligibility = {}
  2390	        # The resources that this core could not release.  A resource
  2391	        # that could not be closed keeps its original reference and its
  2392	        # owner here: it is named in the evidence of the round and it is
  2393	        # never replaced by a copy of an error message.
  2394	        self._retained_resources = []
  2395	        # The cancellations that were observed during the coordination of
  2396	        # the readers.  They are raised after every reader was
  2397	        # coordinated and they never replace an ordinary failure.
  2398	        self._readers_cancelled = []
  2399	        # The cancellations that were observed during the wait of the
  2400	        # original processes.  They are raised after every process was
  2401	        # coordinated.
  2402	        self._processes_cancelled = []
  2403	        # The absolute deadline of the round.  It is bound by the loop
  2404	        # before the job of the round exists and it is the upper bound of
  2405	        # the one cleanup deadline of the round.
  2406	        self._absolute_deadline = None
  2407	        # The ONE cleanup deadline of the round.  It is generated at the
  2408	        # first stop or at the first failure of the cleanup and it is the
  2409	        # same value in every step of the cleanup.
  2410	        self._cleanup_deadline_value = None
  2411	        self._released = False
  2412	        self._abort_reported = False
  2413	        self.broker_identity = {
  2414	            'pid': os.getpid(),
  2415	            'executable': sys.executable,
  2416	            'reported_by_the_broker': True,
  2417	            'identity_source': 'CHILD_CLAIM',
  2418	        }
  2419	
  2420	    # -- the deadlines of the round ---------------------------------------
  2421	    def bind_clock(self, clock):
  2422	        """Bind the clock of the round to this core.
  2423	
  2424	        The core, the loop and the context of the round read the same
  2425	        clock: the absolute deadline of the round and the ONE cleanup
  2426	        deadline are in one domain and a deadline of one domain is never
  2427	        compared with a clock of another one.
  2428	        """
  2429	        if clock is not None:
  2430	            self._clock = clock
  2431	            self.context.clock = clock
  2432	        return self._clock
  2433	
  2434	    def bind_absolute_deadline(self, deadline):
  2435	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to this core.
  2436	
  2437	        The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
  2438	        bound.  It is the upper bound of the one cleanup deadline of the
  2439	        round: the cleanup never outlives the round itself.
  2440	        """
  2441	        value = self.context.bind_absolute_deadline(deadline)
  2442	        self._absolute_deadline = value
  2443	        # The façade of the primitives is bound to the same deadline: its
  2444	        # waits are bounded by the deadline of the round and they never
  2445	        # start a wait with a fresh timeout of their own.
  2446	        binder = getattr(self.win32, 'bind_deadline', None)
  2447	        if callable(binder):
  2448	            try:
  2449	                binder(value, self._clock)
  2450	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2451	                pass
  2452	        return value
  2453	
  2454	    def cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  2455	        """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of this round.
  2456	
  2457	        The deadline is generated once, at the first stop or at the first
  2458	        failure of the cleanup, and it is published **atomically** by the
  2459	        context of the round.  Two callers that entered the cleanup at
  2460	        the same moment therefore see the very same value and the
  2461	        deadline is never generated twice.
  2462	
  2463	        It is the earlier of the absolute deadline of the round and the
  2464	        fixed cleanup budget, measured from the moment of the first
  2465	        cleanup.  Every later call returns the very same value: no layer
  2466	        of the cleanup adds a budget of its own and no layer starts a
  2467	        new wait with a fresh timeout.
  2468	        """
  2469	        value = self.context.cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  2470	        self._cleanup_deadline_value = value
  2471	        return value
  2472	
  2473	    def remaining_cleanup_seconds(self, deadline=None):
  2474	        """The seconds that are left until the cleanup deadline.
  2475	
  2476	        The value is recomputed on every call: a wait that already
  2477	        consumed part of the budget never gets the whole budget again.
  2478	        """
  2479	        return self.context.remaining_cleanup(deadline)
  2480	
  2481	    def check_startup_budget(self, stage):
  2482	        """Check the shared budget of the startup **before** a step.
  2483	
  2484	        A step of the startup of the round - the creation of a process,
  2485	        the assignment of a process to the job, the resume of its
  2486	        thread, the start of a reader and the creation of the next role
  2487	        - checks the shared cancel and the shared deadline of the round
  2488	        before it happens and after it happened.
  2489	
  2490	        A step that runs after the deadline arrived may only clean up:
  2491	        it never resumes a thread and it never creates the next role.
  2492	        """
  2493	        context = self.context
  2494	        if context.cancel.is_set() or context.is_unknown():
  2495	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
  2496	        deadline = context.absolute_deadline
  2497	        if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
  2498	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', stage)
  2499	        return True
  2500	
  2501	    def _retain_resource(self, kind, handle, code=None, owner='CORE_RETAINED'):
  2502	        """Keep the original reference of a resource that was not released."""
  2503	        for item in self._retained_resources:
  2504	            if item['kind'] == kind and item['handle'] is handle:
  2505	                return item
  2506	        item = {'kind': kind, 'handle': handle, 'owner': owner, 'code': code}
  2507	        self._retained_resources.append(item)
  2508	        return item
  2509	
  2510	    def _release_resource(self, kind, handle):
  2511	        """Forget a resource that was released with a confirmed close.
  2512	
  2513	        The **original reference** of a resource is its identity: a
  2514	        handle that was really closed is forgotten whatever name it was
  2515	        retained under.  A resource that was not closed keeps its owner
  2516	        and it stays named: the retained list is never cleared to make a
  2517	        check green.
  2518	        """
  2519	        self._retained_resources = [item for item in self._retained_resources
  2520	                                   if item['handle'] is not handle]
  2521	
  2522	    def _adopt_from_api(self, entries, role):
  2523	        """Take the named handles over from the façade of the primitives.
  2524	
  2525	        The receiver registers the handles and the façade forgets them
  2526	        in **one** atomic step.  A handle that both of them names would
  2527	        have two independent owners and the recovery of the façade could
  2528	        close a handle that a live reader of the round is reading from.
  2529	        A handle that left the façade before its receiver registered it
  2530	        would have no owner at all.
  2531	
  2532	        The façade that does not offer the atomic handover is asked to
  2533	        forget the handles after the registration: the receiver is
  2534	        always registered first, so that a failure in between leaves the
  2535	        handle with **two** owners and never with none.
  2536	        """
  2537	        items = list(entries)
  2538	
  2539	        def register(handles):
  2540	            for handle, kind in handles:
  2541	                self._pending.add(handle, kind, role=role)
  2542	
  2543	        handover = getattr(self.win32, 'handover', None)
  2544	        if callable(handover):
  2545	            handover(items, owner='CORE', register=register)
  2546	            return
  2547	        register(items)
  2548	        forget = getattr(self.win32, 'forget_handle', None)
  2549	        if callable(forget):
  2550	            for handle, _kind in items:
  2551	                forget(handle)
  2552	
  2553	    def _api_retained(self):
  2554	        """The original handles that the Windows façade still holds.
  2555	
  2556	        The façade is the owner of a handle that it could not hand over
  2557	        and it is the owner of the recovery of that handle.  The core
  2558	        keeps the façade, so the core can always reach these handles.
  2559	
  2560	        The names of the handles are the printable ones: the evidence of
  2561	        a round is a document and it never carries a raw handle value.
  2562	        """
  2563	        getter = getattr(self.win32, 'retained_handles', None)
  2564	        if not callable(getter):
  2565	            return []
  2566	        try:
  2567	            entries = list(getter())
  2568	        except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2569	            return []
  2570	        return [{'kind': entry.get('kind'), 'pid': entry.get('pid'),
  2571	                 'stage': entry.get('stage'), 'owner': entry.get('owner'),
  2572	                 'failures': list(entry.get('failures', ())),
  2573	                 'handle_repr': repr(entry.get('handle'))}
  2574	                for entry in entries]
  2575	
  2576	    # -- the arming of the round ------------------------------------------
  2577	    def arm(self):
  2578	        """Create the job object of the round.
  2579	
  2580	        The job is created before any process of the round exists, so that
  2581	        every process of the round can be assigned to it before it runs.
  2582	
  2583	        The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** the
  2584	        creation of the job and **after** it: a job that was created
  2585	        after the deadline of the round arrived is only cleaned up and it
  2586	        is never armed.
  2587	        """
  2588	        if self.state != 'NEW':
  2589	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STATE_INVALID', self.state)
  2590	        self.state = 'VALIDATED'
  2591	        self.check_startup_budget('ARM:JOB')
  2592	        try:
  2593	            self.job = self.win32.create_job()
  2594	        except BrokerError as error:
  2595	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_job', 'code': error.code})
  2596	            self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  2597	            raise
  2598	        self._pending.add(self.job, 'job')
  2599	        self._pending.transfer(self.job, 'CORE')
  2600	        try:
  2601	            self.win32.set_job_kill_on_close(self.job)
  2602	        except BrokerError as error:
  2603	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'set_job_limits', 'code': error.code})
  2604	            closed = self._close_handle('job', self.job)
  2605	            if closed:
  2606	                # The job handle was closed with a confirmed close: it may
  2607	                # be forgotten.
  2608	                self.job = None
  2609	            else:
  2610	                # The job handle was not closed.  The original reference
  2611	                # stays with its owner and it is named: a handle that may
  2612	                # not be closed is never forgotten and it is never
  2613	                # replaced by a copy of an error message.
  2614	                self._retain_resource('job', self.job, error.code)
  2615	            self.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  2616	            raise
  2617	        self.job_kill_on_close = True
  2618	        self.state = 'ARMED'
  2619	        # The budget is checked after the creation of the job: a job that
  2620	        # was created after the deadline arrived is only cleaned up.
  2621	        self.check_startup_budget('ARM:DONE')
  2622	        return self.evidence()
  2623	
  2624	    # -- the launch of the roles ------------------------------------------
  2625	    def launch(self):
  2626	        """Create the roles of the round, one after the other.
  2627	
  2628	        The launch has an independent ``finally``: a failure of a role,
  2629	        a refusal of the broker and a cancellation all release the
  2630	        resources of the round before the failure leaves this method.
  2631	        The cancellation itself is not swallowed: it continues to
  2632	        propagate after the cleanup.
  2633	        """
  2634	        if self.state != 'ARMED':
  2635	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_STATE_INVALID', self.state)
  2636	        try:
  2637	            for role in self.plan.roles:
  2638	                # The shared budget of the startup is checked before
  2639	                # every role: a role that is created after the deadline
  2640	                # of the round arrived is a role that nobody may stop.
  2641	                self.check_startup_budget('ROLE:' + role['role'])
  2642	                self._launch_role(role)
  2643	        except BaseException:
  2644	            # Any failure of the launch, a cancellation included, ends
  2645	            # the round closed: the job, the pipes and the original
  2646	            # handles of this round are released here.
  2647	            self._abort_round('LAUNCH_ABORTED')
  2648	            raise
  2649	        self.state = 'RUNNING'
  2650	        return self.evidence()
  2651	
  2652	    def _launch_role(self, role):
  2653	        name = role['role']
  2654	        owned = [record['pid'] for record in self.records if record['pid']]
  2655	        command = role_command(role, self.run_root, owned)
  2656	        record = {'role': name,
  2657	                  'executable': command['executable'],
  2658	                  'executable_sha256': role['tools'][0]['sha256'],
  2659	                  'arguments': list(command['arguments']),
  2660	                  'working_directory': command['working_directory'],
  2661	                  'log': role['log'],
  2662	                  'created': False, 'assigned': False, 'resumed': False,
  2663	                  'pid': None, 'creation_time': None,
  2664	                  'terminated': False, 'exited': False, 'exit_code': None,
  2665	                  'process_handle_closed': False, 'thread_handle_closed': False,
  2666	                  'stdout_bytes': 0, 'stderr_bytes': 0,
  2667	                  'stdout_overflow': False, 'stderr_overflow': False,
  2668	                  'failure': None,
  2669	                  'pending_handles': []}
  2670	        self.records.append(record)
  2671	        try:
  2672	            self._prepare_directories(role)
  2673	        except OSError as error:
  2674	            record['failure'] = 'BROKER_ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED'
  2675	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'role_directory', 'role': name,
  2676	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  2677	            self._abort_round('ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED')
  2678	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_ROLE_DIRECTORY_FAILED', name)
  2679	        try:
  2680	            # The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** every
  2681	            # pipe: a pipe that is created after the deadline of the round
  2682	            # arrived is a pipe that nobody may close.
  2683	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE:stdout:' + name)
  2684	            stdout_read, stdout_write = self.win32.create_pipe('stdout')
  2685	            # The two ends are handed over to the core in one step: the
  2686	            # façade forgets them and the core registers them, so that
  2687	            # the two tables never name the same handle.
  2688	            self._adopt_from_api([(stdout_read, 'pipe_read'),
  2689	                                  (stdout_write, 'pipe_write')], name)
  2690	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE:stderr:' + name)
  2691	            stderr_read, stderr_write = self.win32.create_pipe('stderr')
  2692	            self._adopt_from_api([(stderr_read, 'pipe_read'),
  2693	                                  (stderr_write, 'pipe_write')], name)
  2694	            self.check_startup_budget('PIPE_DONE:' + name)
  2695	        except BrokerError as error:
  2696	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2697	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_pipe', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
  2698	            self._abort_round('PIPE_FAILED')
  2699	            raise
  2700	        try:
  2701	            # The shared budget of the startup is checked **before** the
  2702	            # creation: a role that is created after the deadline of the
  2703	            # round arrived would be a role that nobody may stop.
  2704	            self.check_startup_budget('CREATE:' + name)
  2705	            created = self.win32.create_process_suspended(
  2706	                executable=command['executable'], arguments=command['arguments'],
  2707	                working_directory=command['working_directory'],
  2708	                stdout_handle=stdout_write, stderr_handle=stderr_write,
  2709	                environment=command['environment'])
  2710	        except BrokerError as error:
  2711	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2712	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
  2713	                                  'code': error.code})
  2714	            # The façade keeps the original handles of a process that it
  2715	            # could not hand over.  They are named here: the core holds
  2716	            # the façade and the recovery of the façade can reach them.
  2717	            retained = self._api_retained()
  2718	            if retained:
  2719	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process_retained',
  2720	                                      'role': name, 'retained': retained})
  2721	            self._abort_round('CREATE_FAILED')
  2722	            raise
  2723	        # The original handles of the process enter the pending table of
  2724	        # the round in the moment of their creation and the core takes
  2725	        # them right away: it is their owner from now on and it closes
  2726	        # them when it releases the records.
  2727	        #
  2728	        # The **eligibility** of the control handle is a callable over
  2729	        # the ORIGINAL record: the pending pass asks it at the moment
  2730	        # of its close and it reads the current exit fact of the
  2731	        # record.  The release of the records asks the very same
  2732	        # callable, so that the record and the pending table never
  2733	        # disagree about the question "may this handle be closed now?".
  2734	        #
  2735	        # The registration happens **before** the check of the shared
  2736	        # budget that follows the creation and before every other step
  2737	        # that may raise.  A process that was created after the deadline
  2738	        # arrived is only cleaned up and it is never owned by nobody:
  2739	        # the lease of the round may end, the ownership of the handles
  2740	        # may not.
  2741	        eligibility = self._process_closeable(record)
  2742	        self._process_eligibility[id(record)] = eligibility
  2743	        self._pending.add(created.process, 'process', role=name,
  2744	                          closeable=eligibility)
  2745	        self._pending.add(created.thread, 'thread', role=name)
  2746	        self._pending.transfer(created.process, 'CORE')
  2747	        self._pending.transfer(created.thread, 'CORE')
  2748	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'process', 'owner': 'CORE'})
  2749	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'thread', 'owner': 'CORE'})
  2750	        record['created'] = True
  2751	        record['pid'] = created.pid
  2752	        record['creation_time'] = created.creation_time
  2753	        record['process_handle'] = created.process
  2754	        record['thread_handle'] = created.thread
  2755	        for failure in getattr(created, 'failures', ()):
  2756	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
  2757	                                  'code': failure})
  2758	        try:
  2759	            # The budget is checked again after the creation: a process
  2760	            # that was created after the deadline arrived is only
  2761	            # cleaned up, it is never assigned and it is never resumed.
  2762	            self.check_startup_budget('CREATED:' + name)
  2763	        except BrokerError as error:
  2764	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2765	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process', 'role': name,
  2766	                                  'code': error.code})
  2767	            # The façade keeps the original handles of a process that it
  2768	            # could not hand over.  They are named here: the core holds
  2769	            # the façade and the recovery of the façade can reach them.
  2770	            retained = self._api_retained()
  2771	            if retained:
  2772	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'create_process_retained',
  2773	                                      'role': name, 'retained': retained})
  2774	            self._abort_round('CREATE_FAILED')
  2775	            raise
  2776	        # The write ends belong to the child now; the broker keeps the read
  2777	        # ends only.  The copies of the broker are closed and they leave
  2778	        # the pending table: they are not pending any more.
  2779	        self._release_pending(stdout_write)
  2780	        self._release_pending(stderr_write)
  2781	        try:
  2782	            # The shared budget is checked before the assignment and
  2783	            # before the resume: a thread that is resumed after the
  2784	            # deadline of the round arrived is a service that runs
  2785	            # outside of the round.
  2786	            self.check_startup_budget('ASSIGN:' + name)
  2787	            self.win32.assign_process_to_job(self.job, created.process)
  2788	        except BrokerError as error:
  2789	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2790	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'assign', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
  2791	            # The process is still suspended: it never ran.  It is stopped
  2792	            # through its own original handle and its exit is confirmed.
  2793	            self._terminate_owned(created.process, record, 'ASSIGN_FAILED')
  2794	            self._abort_round('ASSIGN_FAILED')
  2795	            raise
  2796	        record['assigned'] = True
  2797	        try:
  2798	            self.check_startup_budget('RESUME:' + name)
  2799	            self.win32.resume_thread(created.thread)
  2800	        except BrokerError as error:
  2801	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2802	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'resume', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
  2803	            self._abort_round('RESUME_FAILED')
  2804	            raise
  2805	        record['resumed'] = True
  2806	        try:
  2807	            self.check_startup_budget('READER:' + name)
  2808	            self._start_reader(record, stdout_read, 'stdout')
  2809	            self.check_startup_budget('READER:' + name)
  2810	            self._start_reader(record, stderr_read, 'stderr')
  2811	        except BrokerError as error:
  2812	            record['failure'] = error.code
  2813	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'reader', 'role': name, 'code': error.code})
  2814	            self._abort_round('READER_FAILED')
  2815	            raise
  2816	
  2817	    def _release_pending(self, handle):
  2818	        """Release one pending handle that the child took over."""
  2819	        item = self._pending.take(handle)
  2820	        if item is None:
  2821	            return False
  2822	        try:
  2823	            self.win32.close_handle(handle)
  2824	        except BrokerError as error:
  2825	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2826	                                  'role': item['role'], 'code': error.code})
  2827	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
  2828	            return False
  2829	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  2830	            # A cancellation never becomes an ordinary evidence: it is
  2831	            # named and it is raised, so that the cleanups that follow
  2832	            # it still run.
  2833	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2834	                                  'role': item['role'],
  2835	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  2836	                                  'cancellation': True})
  2837	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
  2838	            raise
  2839	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2840	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_stream', 'kind': item['kind'],
  2841	                                  'role': item['role'],
  2842	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  2843	            self._pending.add(handle, item['kind'], role=item['role'])
  2844	            return False
  2845	        return True
  2846	
  2847	    def _prepare_directories(self, role):
  2848	        """Create the fresh directories of a role.
  2849	
  2850	        A directory that already exists is refused: a stale log, a stale
  2851	        pid lease or a stale sampler output of an earlier round may never
  2852	        be read as the output of this round.
  2853	
  2854	        The user directory of the ComfyUI role is the directory that the
  2855	        plan closed and not a fixed name: the round creates the
  2856	        directory that the command of the role really uses.
  2857	        """
  2858	        name = role['role']
  2859	        if name == 'COMFY':
  2860	            (Path(self.run_root) / 'temp' / 'service-os').mkdir(parents=True,
  2861	                                                                exist_ok=True)
  2862	            (Path(self.run_root) / role['data']['user_directory']).mkdir(
  2863	                parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  2864	        elif name == 'SAMPLER':
  2865	            (Path(self.run_root) / 'temp' / 'resource-sampler').mkdir(
  2866	                parents=True, exist_ok=False)
  2867	
  2868	    def _start_reader(self, record, handle, stream):
  2869	        """Hand the read end of a pipe over to its reader.
  2870	
  2871	        The reader is the owner of the read end from now on: the
  2872	        handover changes the owner of the handle and nothing else.  The
  2873	        handle stays in the pending table of the round until it is
  2874	        really closed, and the reader closes it when it is stopped.
  2875	
  2876	        The reader **object** is registered as the owner of the handle
  2877	        and not only its name: the pending pass asks the reader whether
  2878	        it is still alive before it closes the read end under it.
  2879	        """
  2880	        reader = _BoundedStreamReader(self.win32, handle, limit=MAX_STREAM_BYTES,
  2881	                                      name=record['role'] + '-' + stream)
  2882	        self.readers.append(reader)
  2883	        self._pending.transfer(handle, 'READER', owner_ref=reader)
  2884	        record['pending_handles'].append({'kind': 'pipe_read',
  2885	                                          'stream': stream, 'owner': 'READER'})
  2886	        reader.start()
  2887	
  2888	    @staticmethod
  2889	    def _process_closeable(record):
  2890	        """The close eligibility of the control handle of a process.
  2891	
  2892	        The control handle of a process is closed **only** when the exit
  2893	        of that process was really confirmed.  The fact comes from the
  2894	        **record** of the process and it is not a name of a state: a
  2895	        process whose exit was not confirmed may still be alive and its
  2896	        control handle is the only handle that may stop it.
  2897	
  2898	        The eligibility is a **callable** over the original record and
  2899	        never a frozen copy of a fact: the pending pass asks it at the
  2900	        moment of its close and it reads the **current** exit fact of
  2901	        the record.  A pending entry that froze the answer at the
  2902	        moment of the creation of the process would never close the
  2903	        handle of a process whose exit was confirmed later.
  2904	
  2905	        The same eligibility is asked by the release of the records and
  2906	        by the pending pass of the round: the pending pass may not
  2907	        close behind the back of the owner of the handle.
  2908	        """
  2909	        def eligible():
  2910	            return bool(record.get('exited'))
  2911	        return eligible
  2912	
  2913	    @staticmethod
  2914	    def _process_exit_confirmed(record):
  2915	        """Whether the exit of the process of a record was confirmed.
  2916	
  2917	        The fact is read from the **original** record and it is asked at
  2918	        the moment of the decision: a record whose exit was confirmed
  2919	        after the creation of the process is a record whose control
  2920	        handle may be closed.
  2921	        """
  2922	        return bool(record.get('exited'))
  2923	
  2924	    def _process_eligible(self, record):
  2925	        """Whether the control handle of the process of a record may be closed.
  2926	
  2927	        The **very same** callable that the pending table of the round
  2928	        holds is asked: the record and the pending table never disagree
  2929	        about the current exit fact of the process.  A record without
  2930	        an eligibility of its own is asked directly: the fact comes
  2931	        from the record and it is never a name of a state.
  2932	        """
  2933	        eligibility = self._process_eligibility.get(id(record))
  2934	        if eligibility is None:
  2935	            return self._process_exit_confirmed(record)
  2936	        return bool(eligibility())
  2937	
  2938	    def _close_handle(self, kind, handle):
  2939	        """Close one owned handle and name a failure of the close.
  2940	
  2941	        A handle that was closed leaves the pending table of the round
  2942	        and it leaves the retained resources of the round: a handle that
  2943	        is really closed is never named as a resource any more.  A
  2944	        handle that could not be closed stays pending: it is a resource
  2945	        that this round still owns and it is named.
  2946	
  2947	        A **confirmed** close is notified through the **one** notification
  2948	        of the round: the job of the round, the records of the round, the
  2949	        reader that owns the handle and the retained resources of the
  2950	        round learn the very same fact, and every owner of a handle that
  2951	        was really closed knows that it must never close it again.
  2952	
  2953	        A **cancellation** in the middle of a close is never turned into
  2954	        an ordinary evidence: it is named and it is raised, so that the
  2955	        finalizer that follows it still runs and the cancellation
  2956	        continues to propagate after the cleanup.
  2957	        """
  2958	        entry = self._pending.find(handle)
  2959	        try:
  2960	            self.win32.close_handle(handle)
  2961	        except BrokerError as error:
  2962	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
  2963	                                  'code': error.code})
  2964	            return False
  2965	        except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  2966	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
  2967	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  2968	                                  'cancellation': True})
  2969	            raise
  2970	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  2971	            self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_handle', 'kind': kind,
  2972	                                  'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  2973	            return False
  2974	        self._pending.take(handle)
  2975	        # The handle is really closed: every owner of it is notified
  2976	        # through the one notification of the round.
  2977	        self._note_closed_handle(entry if entry is not None
  2978	                                 else {'handle': handle, 'kind': kind})
  2979	        return True
  2980	
  2981	    def _close_readers(self):
  2982	        """Stop the readers of the children and close their read handles.
  2983	
  2984	        The wait of a reader is bounded by the ONE cleanup deadline of the
  2985	        round and its state is recorded.  A reader that is still blocked
  2986	        when the wait is over is a *fact* of this round and it is
  2987	        reported as such: a daemon thread that is still running is not a
  2988	        finished thread, and the read end of such a reader is not closed
  2989	        under it.
  2990	
  2991	        Every reader is independent of the others: a reader that could
  2992	        not be joined never skips the coordination of the readers that
  2993	        follow it, and a reader whose thread never started is never
  2994	        joined at all.  A read end that was closed with a confirmed
  2995	        close is never closed a second time.
  2996	        """
  2997	        for reader in self.readers:
  2998	            try:
  2999	                self._close_one_reader(reader)
  3000	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  3001	                # A cancellation never skips the readers that follow it:
  3002	                # it is named and it is raised after every reader was
  3003	                # coordinated.
  3004	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_readers',
  3005	                                      'name': reader.name,
  3006	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  3007	                                      'cancellation': True})
  3008	                self._readers_cancelled.append(error)
  3009	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  3010	                self.failures.append({'stage': 'close_readers',
  3011	                                      'name': reader.name,
  3012	                                      'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  3013	        if self._readers_cancelled:
  3014	            raise self._readers_cancelled[0]
  3015	
  3016	    def _close_one_reader(self, reader):
  3017	        """Coordinate one reader of the round on its own.
  3018	
  3019	        The read end of a reader is closed **only** when the reader
  3020	        really stopped.  Every branch of the coordination asks the
  3021	        reader whether it is still alive: a wait that was cancelled and
  3022	        a join that failed are not a proof that the reader ended, and
  3023	        the read end of a live reader is never closed under it.
  3024	
  3025	        A cancellation in the middle of the coordination never skips the
  3026	        readers that follow it and it never skips the protection of the
  3027	        read end of the reader that is still alive.
  3028	        """
  3029	        if reader.handle is None:
  3030	            # The read end of this reader was closed with a confirmed
  3031	            # close already.
  3032	            return
  3033	        state = {'name': reader.name,
  3034	                 'finished': reader.finished.is_set(),
  3035	                 'joined': False,
