  4502	    #: first use and it is never shared between two endpoints.
  4503	    _admission_lock = None
  4504	
  4505	    def __init__(self, file_object, *, name, api=None, clock=None,
  4506	                 handle=None, context=None):
  4507	        self._file = file_object
  4508	        self._name = name
  4509	        self._api = api
  4510	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  4511	        self._closed = False
  4512	        self._reader = None
  4513	        self._writer = None
  4514	        self._retained = []
  4515	        self._deadline = None
  4516	        self._write_cancel = None
  4517	        self._write_deadline = None
  4518	        # The lock of the **admission** of the endpoint.  The check of
  4519	        # the cancel flag, of the deadline of the round and the entry of
  4520	        # the request into the worker are one atomic step under it.
  4521	        self._admission_lock = threading.RLock()
  4522	        # The ONE context of the round.  The endpoint reads the absolute
  4523	        # deadline of the round from it and it never keeps a budget of
  4524	        # its own.
  4525	        self._context = context
  4526	        if handle is None:
  4527	            import msvcrt
  4528	            handle = msvcrt.get_osfhandle(file_object.fileno())
  4529	        self._handle = handle
  4530	        self.facts = {'name': name, 'owner': 'WINDOWS_ENDPOINT'}
  4531	
  4532	    def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
  4533	        """Bind an absolute deadline to this endpoint.
  4534	
  4535	        The deadline may only shorten the deadline that is already
  4536	        bound and it is read again on every slice.
  4537	        """
  4538	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  4539	        if value is None:
  4540	            return self._deadline
  4541	        if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
  4542	            return self._deadline
  4543	        self._deadline = value
  4544	        return value
  4545	
  4546	    def bind_context(self, context):
  4547	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to this endpoint."""
  4548	        if context is not None:
  4549	            self._context = context
  4550	        return self._context
  4551	
  4552	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  4553	        """The deadline that this call really uses.
  4554	
  4555	        The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
  4556	        endpoint, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
  4557	        cleanup deadline of the round are **one** value: the earliest of
  4558	        the four.  The endpoint never keeps a deadline of its own and it
  4559	        never treats a missing context as an unlimited budget.
  4560	
  4561	        The published cleanup deadline is part of it: a round that
  4562	        entered its cleanup publishes one deadline and the endpoint
  4563	        that reads the context afterwards is bound by it.  An endpoint
  4564	        that still waited with the long lease of the round would
  4565	        outlive the only cleanup budget of the round.
  4566	        """
  4567	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  4568	        bound = self._deadline
  4569	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
  4570	            value = bound
  4571	        context = self._context
  4572	        if context is not None:
  4573	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  4574	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
  4575	                value = absolute
  4576	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  4577	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
  4578	                value = cleanup
  4579	        return value
  4580	
  4581	    def _admission_lock_for(self):
  4582	        """The lock of the admission of **this** endpoint.
  4583	
  4584	        The lock is created **per instance** and it is created once.
  4585	        An endpoint that was built without its constructor - a fixture
  4586	        of the offline validation - therefore still has a **real**
  4587	        admission: the check of the cancel flag and of the deadline is
  4588	        never skipped and it is never permissive.  The creation of the
  4589	        lock is guarded by one lock of the module, so that two threads
  4590	        that arrive at the same time never build two locks for the same
  4591	        endpoint.
  4592	        """
  4593	        lock = self.__dict__.get('_admission_lock')
  4594	        if lock is not None:
  4595	            return lock
  4596	        with _ADMISSION_LOCK_INIT:
  4597	            lock = self.__dict__.get('_admission_lock')
  4598	            if lock is None:
  4599	                lock = threading.RLock()
  4600	                self.__dict__['_admission_lock'] = lock
  4601	        return lock
  4602	
  4603	    def _admission(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  4604	        """The admission of one generation of this endpoint.
  4605	
  4606	        The admission is asked **before** every side effect: before the
  4607	        worker of the endpoint is started, before a request is
  4608	        submitted and before the primitive is called.  A call that
  4609	        entered when the round was already expired or already cancelled
  4610	        is refused there: it never starts a worker, it never submits a
  4611	        request and it never calls the primitive.
  4612	
  4613	        The check and the admission of the generation are **one atomic
  4614	        step** under the lock of the endpoint: a cancellation that
  4615	        arrives between the check and the submit may not be answered by
  4616	        a request that was submitted anyway.
  4617	        """
  4618	        with self._admission_lock_for():
  4619	            return self._admission_locked(deadline, cancel)
  4620	
  4621	    def _admission_locked(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  4622	        """The admission of one generation, under the lock of the endpoint.
  4623	
  4624	        The caller holds the lock of the admission: the check of the
  4625	        cancel flag, of the deadline of the round and of the context of
  4626	        the round and the decision of the admission are **one** atomic
  4627	        step.  The **final** admission of a generation is asked here,
  4628	        immediately before the submission of the request: a
  4629	        cancellation or an expiry that was published **before** that
  4630	        admission causes **zero** primitive calls.
  4631	        """
  4632	        effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  4633	        if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  4634	            # The cancellation of the round arrived first: the
  4635	            # request is refused and no side effect happens.
  4636	            raise _cancel_source_error(
  4637	                BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name), cancel)
  4638	        context = self._context
  4639	        if context is not None and context.cancel.is_set():
  4640	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
  4641	        if effective is not None and self._clock() >= effective:
  4642	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
  4643	        return effective
  4644	
  4645	    def _remaining(self, deadline=None):
  4646	        """The seconds that are left until the deadline of this endpoint."""
  4647	        value = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  4648	        if value is None:
  4649	            return CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
  4650	        return max(0.0, value - self._clock())
  4651	
  4652	    def _wait_slice(self, effective=None):
  4653	        """The seconds that the next wait of this endpoint may ask for.
  4654	
  4655	        The wait is the ordinary poll slice of the package, cropped to
  4656	        the time that is really left until the **current** effective
  4657	        deadline of the round.  A wait never asks for more than the
  4658	        round allows and it never asks for a positive floor beyond it:
  4659	        an expired round is refused before the wait and a round that is
  4660	        about to expire waits only for the time that is left.
  4661	        """
  4662	        value = POLL_SLICE_SECONDS
  4663	        if effective is None:
  4664	            effective = self._effective_deadline()
  4665	        if effective is not None:
  4666	            value = max(0.0, min(value, effective - self._clock()))
  4667	        return value
  4668	
  4669	    def _start(self, kind, action, deadline=None):
  4670	        """Create and start one worker of this endpoint.
  4671	
  4672	        The worker is registered with the endpoint **before** it is
  4673	        started.  An endpoint that owns a worker only after the start
  4674	        returned has no owner at all for a worker whose start did not
  4675	        return, and the close of the endpoint could close the file
  4676	        under a worker that is about to read from it.
  4677	
  4678	        The wait for the handle of the thread of the worker is bounded
  4679	        by the **current** remaining budget of the round.  It never
  4680	        opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed number of
  4681	        seconds.
  4682	        """
  4683	        worker = _WindowsPipeWorker(self._api, self._handle, kind=kind,
  4684	                                    action=action, clock=self._clock,
  4685	                                    context=self._context,
  4686	                                    name='lvw-native-custody-pipe-' + kind)
  4687	        # The pre-registration: the endpoint is the owner of the worker
  4688	        # before the thread of the worker exists.
  4689	        if kind == 'read':
  4690	            self._reader = worker
  4691	        else:
  4692	            self._writer = worker
  4693	        try:
  4694	            worker.start()
  4695	        except BaseException:
  4696	            # The start of the worker did not return.  The worker keeps
  4697	            # its owner and it may still come up: it is never forgotten
  4698	            # and it is never closed under.
  4699	            self._retained.append(worker)
  4700	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
  4701	            self.facts['worker_alive'] = True
  4702	            raise
  4703	        # The worker acquires the handle of its own thread before it may
  4704	        # be used.  The wait for it is bounded by the remaining budget of
  4705	        # the round.
  4706	        worker.wait_ready(self._remaining(deadline))
  4707	        return worker
  4708	
  4709	    def _await(self, worker, request, deadline, cancel):
  4710	        """Wait for the completion of one worker, bounded and cancellable.
  4711	
  4712	        The **final** admission of the generation happens here: it is
  4713	        asked after the worker is ready and it is asked **immediately
  4714	        before** the submission of the request, under the lock of the
  4715	        admission.  That is the **linearization point** of the request:
  4716	        a cancellation or an expiry that was published **before** it
  4717	        causes **zero** primitive calls and a cancellation that arrives
  4718	        **after** it follows the bounded cancellation below.  No claim
  4719	        is made that an operating system call that already began may be
  4720	        undone atomically.
  4721	
  4722	        The cancel flag and the deadline of the call travel with the
  4723	        request: the worker asks them itself before it enters the
  4724	        operation and a request whose admission was refused never calls
  4725	        the primitive.
  4726	
  4727	        The deadline that this wait really uses is **recomputed on every
  4728	        slice**: a deadline that was bound after the wait started still
  4729	        tightens the wait that is running.  A wait that carries no
  4730	        deadline yet observes the deadline as soon as it is bound, and
  4731	        the cancel flag ends it in the meantime.
  4732	
  4733	        The **result of the generation** wins over the cancellation and
  4734	        over a new timeout.  A fault that the worker really published
  4735	        before the cancellation of the round is the first error of the
  4736	        round: the cancellation of a later close may not replace it and
  4737	        it may not turn it into a benign cancellation.  The order of the
  4738	        two facts is the order of the events of the worker and it is
  4739	        never a guess of the scheduler.
  4740	        """
  4741	        with self._admission_lock_for():
  4742	            # The final admission of the generation and the submission
  4743	            # of the request are **one** atomic step.
  4744	            effective = self._admission_locked(deadline, cancel)
  4745	            generation = worker.submit(request, cancel=cancel,
  4746	                                       deadline=effective)
  4747	        while True:
  4748	            effective = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  4749	            if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  4750	                self._raise_cancellation(worker, generation, effective, cancel)
  4751	            context = self._context
  4752	            if context is not None and context.cancel.is_set():
  4753	                # The **original** context of the round is noticed here
  4754	                # as well: the flag of the call and the flag of the
  4755	                # channel are not the only latches of the round.
  4756	                self._raise_cancellation(worker, generation, effective,
  4757	                                        context.cancel)
  4758	            if effective is not None and self._clock() >= effective:
  4759	                # Before a **new** timeout is decided, the published
  4760	                # original error of the **same** generation is
  4761	                # inspected: it remains the primary error of the round
  4762	                # and the timeout is a **parallel** fact.
  4763	                outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4764	                if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4765	                    raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome)
  4766	                try:
  4767	                    self._cancel(worker, effective)
  4768	                except BrokerError as error:
  4769	                    # The coordination failed: the published original
  4770	                    # error of the generation is inspected **again**
  4771	                    # before the failure of the coordination is
  4772	                    # decided, and the failure of the coordination
  4773	                    # stays a parallel fact.
  4774	                    outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4775	                    if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4776	                        raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome, error)
  4777	                    raise
  4778	                # Again after the coordination: the outcome of the
  4779	                # generation is immutable and it is never rewritten by
  4780	                # the timeout.
  4781	                outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4782	                if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4783	                    raise self._parallel_timeout(outcome)
  4784	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
  4785	            if worker.wait_done(generation, self._wait_slice(effective)):
  4786	                break
  4787	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4788	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4789	            raise self._outcome_error(outcome)
  4790	        if worker.error:
  4791	            raise BrokerError(worker.error, self._name)
  4792	        return worker.result
  4793	
  4794	    @staticmethod
  4795	    def _parallel_timeout(outcome, parallel=None):
  4796	        """The published failure of a generation with the timeout beside it.
  4797	
  4798	        The **original** error object is the error of the round: a
  4799	        timeout that arrives after the failure was really published is
  4800	        a **parallel** fact and it never replaces the failure.
  4801	        """
  4802	        error = _WindowsPipeEndpoint._outcome_error(outcome)
  4803	        try:
  4804	            error.lvw_parallel_timeout = {
  4805	                'code': 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT',
  4806	                'generation': outcome.get('generation'),
  4807	                'sequence': outcome.get('sequence'),
  4808	                'cancel_sequence': outcome.get('cancel_sequence'),
  4809	                'coordination_error': (type(parallel).__name__
  4810	                                       if parallel is not None else None),
  4811	            }
  4812	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4813	            pass
  4814	        return error
  4815	
  4816	    @staticmethod
  4817	    def _outcome_error(outcome):
  4818	        """The error of one published outcome of a worker.
  4819	
  4820	        The **original** error object is raised when the worker kept
  4821	        it: the object is the failure and the code is only its name.  A
  4822	        worker that kept only a code raises the stable code of the
  4823	        broker.
  4824	        """
  4825	        original = outcome.get('error_object')
  4826	        if original is not None:
  4827	            return original
  4828	        return BrokerError(outcome.get('error'), 'WORKER_OUTCOME')
  4829	
  4830	    def _raise_cancellation(self, worker, generation, effective, cancel):
  4831	        """Raise the cancellation of this generation of the worker.
  4832	
  4833	        The outcome of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
  4834	        cancelled and it is asked again after the worker really stopped.
  4835	        A failure of this generation is the **first error** of the
  4836	        round: a cancellation that arrives afterwards may never replace
  4837	        it and it may never turn it into a benign cancellation.  The
  4838	        cancellation stays a **parallel** fact and it is named next to
  4839	        the failure.
  4840	
  4841	        A **failed** coordination - a cancellation that could not be
  4842	        confirmed or a join that did not end - is a parallel fact as
  4843	        well: it never replaces the published failure of the
  4844	        generation and it never hides it.
  4845	
  4846	        The error of the cancellation carries the owner of the flag: the
  4847	        origin of the cancellation is named at the place where the error
  4848	        is really raised and never by a caller of it.
  4849	        """
  4850	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4851	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4852	            raise self._parallel_fault(outcome, cancel)
  4853	        coordination_error = None
  4854	        try:
  4855	            self._cancel(worker, effective)
  4856	        except BrokerError as error:
  4857	            coordination_error = error
  4858	        outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
  4859	        if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
  4860	            raise self._parallel_fault(outcome, cancel, coordination_error)
  4861	        if coordination_error is not None:
  4862	            raise coordination_error
  4863	        raise _cancel_source_error(
  4864	            BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name), cancel)
  4865	
  4866	    @staticmethod
  4867	    def _parallel_fault(outcome, cancel, coordination_error=None):
  4868	        """The failure of a generation with the cancellation beside it.
  4869	
  4870	        The failure is the error of the round.  The cancellation that
  4871	        was requested at the same moment is a **parallel** fact: it is
  4872	        named next to the failure and it never replaces it.  A
  4873	        coordination that failed is a parallel fact as well.
  4874	        """
  4875	        error = _WindowsPipeEndpoint._outcome_error(outcome)
  4876	        if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  4877	            source = None
  4878	            owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
  4879	            if callable(owner):
  4880	                try:
  4881	                    source = getattr(owner(), 'owner', None)
  4882	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4883	                    source = None
  4884	            try:
  4885	                error.lvw_parallel_cancellation = {
  4886	                    'code': 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED',
  4887	                    'source': source,
  4888	                    'generation': outcome.get('generation'),
  4889	                    'sequence': outcome.get('sequence'),
  4890	                    'cancel_sequence': outcome.get('cancel_sequence'),
  4891	                    'coordination_error': (type(coordination_error).__name__
  4892	                                           if coordination_error is not None
  4893	                                           else None),
  4894	                }
  4895	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  4896	                pass
  4897	        return error
  4898	
  4899	    def _cancel(self, worker, deadline=None):
  4900	        """Cancel one worker and prove that it really stopped."""
  4901	        state = worker.request_cancel(self._effective_deadline(deadline))
  4902	        if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
  4903	            # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner reference and
  4904	            # the round becomes unknown.  It is never terminated blindly
  4905	            # and it is never waited for without a bound.
  4906	            self._retained.append(worker)
  4907	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
  4908	            self.facts['worker_alive'] = True
  4909	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_NOT_CONFIRMED',
  4910	                              '%s:%s' % (self._name, state))
  4911	        if not worker.joined:
  4912	            self._retained.append(worker)
  4913	            self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
  4914	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCEL_NOT_CONFIRMED', self._name)
  4915	
  4916	    def read(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  4917	        """Read one block of the pipe, bounded by the deadline.
  4918	
  4919	        The admission of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
  4920	        started and before the request is submitted: a read that enters
  4921	        when the round is already expired or already cancelled performs
  4922	        **no** primitive call at all.
  4923	        """
  4924	        effective = self._admission(deadline, cancel)
  4925	        if self._reader is None:
  4926	            self._start('read', self._read_block, effective)
  4927	        return self._await(self._reader, None, deadline, cancel)
  4928	
  4929	    def write(self, data, deadline=None, cancel=None):
  4930	        """Write the whole block, bounded by the deadline.
  4931	
  4932	        The admission of the generation is asked **before** the worker is
  4933	        started and before the request is submitted: a write that enters
  4934	        when the round is already expired or already cancelled performs
  4935	        **no** primitive call at all.
  4936	        """
  4937	        effective = self._admission(deadline, cancel)
  4938	        if self._writer is None:
  4939	            self._start('write', self._write_block, effective)
  4940	        # The cancel flag and the deadline of this write are published
  4941	        # for the partial write: every segment of a partial write checks
  4942	        # the cancel flag and the deadline of the round.  They are not
  4943	        # cleared when the write ends: a segment that is still in flight
  4944	        # inside the worker has to observe the cancel of this write.
  4945	        self._write_cancel = cancel
  4946	        self._write_deadline = effective
  4947	        return self._await(self._writer, bytes(data), deadline, cancel)
  4948	
  4949	    def _read_block(self, request):
  4950	        return self._api.read_stream(self._handle, PIPE_READ_BYTES)
  4951	
  4952	    def _current_write_deadline(self):
  4953	        """The deadline that the next segment of a write really uses.
  4954	
  4955	        The value is read **again** for every segment of a partial
  4956	        write.  A copy of the deadline that was taken when the write
  4957	        started would let the write continue past a deadline that the
  4958	        round published in the meantime, and the shared context of the
  4959	        round is the only authority of the deadline.
  4960	
  4961	        The **published cleanup deadline** is part of it: a write that
  4962	        is still running when the round entered its cleanup is bounded by
  4963	        the cleanup budget of the round and not by the long lease.
  4964	        """
  4965	        value = self._write_deadline
  4966	        bound = self._deadline
  4967	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
  4968	            value = bound
  4969	        context = self._context
  4970	        if context is not None:
  4971	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  4972	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
  4973	                value = absolute
  4974	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  4975	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
  4976	                value = cleanup
  4977	        return value
  4978	
  4979	    def _write_block(self, request):
  4980	        """Write the whole block, report the number of the written bytes.
  4981	
  4982	        Windows may write less than the block that it was given.  The
  4983	        endpoint repeats the write until the whole block was written or
  4984	        until the write fails with a named error.  **Every segment** of
  4985	        the partial write checks the cancel flag and the deadline of the
  4986	        round: a write that is not bounded is not a write.
  4987	        """
  4988	        data = bytes(request)
  4989	        total = 0
  4990	        while total < len(data):
  4991	            cancel = self._write_cancel
  4992	            if cancel is not None and cancel.is_set():
  4993	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', self._name)
  4994	            deadline = self._current_write_deadline()
  4995	            if deadline is not None and self._clock() >= deadline:
  4996	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', self._name)
  4997	            count = self._api.write_stream(self._handle, data[total:])
  4998	            if count is None or count <= 0:
  4999	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STREAM_WRITE_FAILED',
  5000	                                  '%s:written=%d' % (self._name, total))
  5001	            total += count
  5002	        return total
  5003	
  5004	    def close(self):
  5005	        """Close the original handle of this endpoint, exactly once.
  5006	
  5007	        The close stops the workers from accepting a new task first, it
  5008	        asks for the cancellation of the operation that is in flight and
  5009	        it joins the workers with a bounded wait.  A worker that did not
  5010	        stop keeps its owner reference: the file is never closed under a
  5011	        worker that is still inside its operation.
  5012	
  5013	        The close is **committed** only when it really happened.  A
  5014	        close that failed keeps the file and its owner: the endpoint
  5015	        may try it again inside the deadline of the round and it is
  5016	        never marked as closed irreversibly.  A close of an endpoint
  5017	        that is already closed keeps the fact that it is closed: the
  5018	        second close is idempotent and it never turns a proven close
  5019	        into a missing one.
  5020	
  5021	        The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
  5022	        worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
  5023	        released is not retained any more, and a worker whose handle of
  5024	        its thread could not be closed is retained again and it may be
  5025	        released by a later close.  A closed file never locks the
  5026	        recovery of the handle of a thread.
  5027	        """
  5028	        deadline = self._effective_deadline()
  5029	        alive = False
  5030	        retained = []
  5031	        for worker in (self._reader, self._writer):
  5032	            if worker is None:
  5033	                continue
  5034	            worker.stop_accepting()
  5035	            worker.request_cancel(deadline)
  5036	            if not worker.join_bounded(self._remaining(deadline)):
  5037	                # The worker did not stop: it keeps its owner and the
  5038	                # file is never closed under it.
  5039	                alive = True
  5040	                retained.append(worker)
  5041	                continue
  5042	            # The thread of the worker really ended: the original handle
  5043	            # of the thread may be released now.  A handle that could
  5044	            # not be closed keeps its owner and a later close may try it
  5045	            # again.
  5046	            if not worker.release_thread_handle():
  5047	                retained.append(worker)
  5048	        # The state of the workers is **recomputed** on every close: a
  5049	        # worker that really ended and whose handle of its thread was
  5050	        # released is not retained any more.  A closed file never locks
  5051	        # the recovery of the handle of a thread.
  5052	        self._retained = retained
  5053	        self.facts['retained_workers'] = len(self._retained)
  5054	        self.facts['worker_alive'] = alive
  5055	        self.facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = all(
  5056	            worker.thread_handle_released
  5057	            for worker in (self._reader, self._writer) if worker is not None)
  5058	        if alive:
  5059	            # A live worker keeps the file: the file is never closed
  5060	            # under a worker that is still inside its operation.
  5061	            self.facts['closed'] = False
  5062	            return self.facts
  5063	        if self._closed:
  5064	            self.facts['closed'] = True
  5065	            self.facts['already_closed'] = True
  5066	            return self.facts
  5067	        try:
  5068	            self._file.close()
  5069	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5070	            self.facts['closed'] = False
  5071	            self.facts['error_type'] = type(error).__name__
  5072	            return self.facts
  5073	        self._closed = True
  5074	        self.facts['closed'] = True
  5075	        return self.facts
  5076	
  5077	
  5078	def _make_endpoint(file_object, name, *, api=None, clock=None, context=None):
  5079	    """The endpoint of one end of the private pipe of this host.
  5080	
  5081	    The Windows host gets the supervised and cancellable endpoint and
  5082	    every other host gets the non blocking endpoint of the original
  5083	    descriptor.  The choice is made by the host and never by a string
  5084	    that a caller could set: a platform that is not Windows never loads
  5085	    the Windows table.
  5086	
  5087	    The Windows endpoint needs the primitive table of the host, because
  5088	    the cancellation of a synchronous operation needs the original
  5089	    handle of the thread that performs it.  The table is loaded only on
  5090	    Windows.
  5091	
  5092	    The ONE context of the round is handed to the endpoint: the
  5093	    endpoint reads the absolute deadline of the round from it and it
  5094	    never keeps a budget of its own.  The context is bound to the
  5095	    **primitive table** as well when the endpoint owns the table: a
  5096	    table that does not know the context of the round would open a
  5097	    budget of its own.
  5098	    """
  5099	    if os.name == 'nt':
  5100	        if api is None:
  5101	            api = WindowsApi()
  5102	        binder = getattr(api, 'bind_context', None)
  5103	        if callable(binder) and context is not None:
  5104	            try:
  5105	                binder(context)
  5106	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5107	                pass
  5108	        return _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, api=api, clock=clock,
  5109	                                    context=context)
  5110	    return _PosixPipeEndpoint(file_object, name=name, clock=clock)
  5111	
  5112	
  5113	# -- the private identity of a cancellation of the round --------------------
  5114	
  5115	class _CloseSource:
  5116	    """The private identity of one close of one channel.
  5117	
  5118	    The identity is an **object**: two closes of the same channel never
  5119	    share it and the owner of a cancellation is compared by identity
  5120	    and never by the name of a code.  The sequence is the order of the
  5121	    closes of the channel and it is part of the evidence.
  5122	    """
  5123	
  5124	    def __init__(self, sequence):
  5125	        self.sequence = sequence
  5126	        self.owner = 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'
  5127	
  5128	
  5129	class _ChannelCancel:
  5130	    """The cancel flag of one channel, with the owner that set it.
  5131	
  5132	    The flag names the **owner** that set it: a cancellation that the
  5133	    close of this channel initiated is provable and a cancellation that
  5134	    another owner initiated is not.  The owner is registered in the
  5135	    same atomic step as the setting of the flag and it is registered
  5136	    **before** the flag is set, so that a reader can never see the flag
  5137	    set without its owner.
  5138	
  5139	    The **first** owner wins: a flag that another owner already set is
  5140	    never re-labelled by a later one.  A cancellation that this channel
  5141	    did not initiate is therefore never taken for a cancellation of the
  5142	    close of this channel.
  5143	    """
  5144	
  5145	    def __init__(self):
  5146	        self._event = threading.Event()
  5147	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
  5148	        self._owner = None
  5149	
  5150	    def is_set(self):
  5151	        return self._event.is_set()
  5152	
  5153	    def set(self, owner=None):
  5154	        """Set the flag and name the owner that set it.
  5155	
  5156	        The owner is written under the lock of the flag and it is
  5157	        written **before** the flag is set: the registration of the
  5158	        owner is never later than the cancellation itself.
  5159	        """
  5160	        with self._lock:
  5161	            if not self._event.is_set():
  5162	                if owner is not None:
  5163	                    self._owner = owner
  5164	                self._event.set()
  5165	
  5166	    def owner(self):
  5167	        """The owner that set the flag, or None when it named none."""
  5168	        with self._lock:
  5169	            return self._owner
  5170	
  5171	    def wait(self, timeout=None):
  5172	        return self._event.wait(timeout)
  5173	
  5174	
  5175	def _cancel_source_error(error, cancel):
  5176	    """Stamp the owner of a cancel flag on the error of a cancellation.
  5177	
  5178	    The owner is read from the flag **at the origin of the error**: the
  5179	    error itself carries the identity of the owner that really set the
  5180	    flag and it never carries a claim of a caller.  A flag that names
  5181	    no owner leaves the error untouched: an unknown source is never
  5182	    taken for a known one.
  5183	    """
  5184	    owner = getattr(cancel, 'owner', None)
  5185	    if not callable(owner):
  5186	        return error
  5187	    try:
  5188	        source = owner()
  5189	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5190	        return error
  5191	    if source is None:
  5192	        return error
  5193	    try:
  5194	        error.lvw_cancel_source = source
  5195	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5196	        pass
  5197	    return error
  5198	
  5199	
  5200	# -- the private control channel -------------------------------------------
  5201	
  5202	class StreamChannel:
  5203	    """The private control channel of the broker.
  5204	
  5205	    The channel is the broker's own standard input and output.  It is
  5206	    bounded three times: one frame is at most 32 KiB, the channel carries
  5207	    at most 1024 frames and the text of the children is bounded by the
  5208	    core.
  5209	
  5210	    The read side is drained by one background thread, so the broker never
  5211	    blocks on the read.  A parent that stops talking therefore cannot hold
  5212	    the deadline of the round: the deadline is checked by the broker
  5213	    itself.
  5214	
  5215	    The channel owns the original handles of its two ends.  A read and a
  5216	    write are bounded by an absolute deadline and by a cancel flag, so a
  5217	    peer that never reads can never hold the supervisor of the round.
  5218	    """
  5219	
  5220	    def __init__(self, reader, writer, *, max_frames=MAX_FRAMES,
  5221	                 max_frame_bytes=MAX_FRAME_BYTES, name='channel',
  5222	                 read_endpoint=None, write_endpoint=None, clock=None, api=None,
  5223	                 context=None, custody=None):
  5224	        self._queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=max_frames)
  5225	        self._max_frames = max_frames
  5226	        self._max_frame_bytes = max_frame_bytes
  5227	        self._count = 0
  5228	        self._overflow = False
  5229	        self._overflow_facts = {}
  5230	        self._eof = False
  5231	        self._error = None
  5232	        self._buffer = b''
  5233	        self._deadline = None
  5234	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  5235	        # The cancel flag of the channel names the owner that set it:
  5236	        # only a cancellation that the close of **this** channel
  5237	        # initiated is provable and only a provable one is a normal
  5238	        # fact of the close.
  5239	        self._cancel = _ChannelCancel()
  5240	        # The private state of the close of this channel: the identity
  5241	        # of the close that is running now and the named fact of the
  5242	        # cancellation that this close really initiated.
  5243	        self._close_source = None
  5244	        self._close_sequence = 0
  5245	        self._close_cancellation = None
  5246	        # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
  5247	        # failure of the reader: it is a parallel fact and it never
  5248	        # replaces the failure.
  5249	        self._parallel_cancellation = None
  5250	        self.reader_alive = None
  5251	        self.reader_finished = None
  5252	        # The ONE context of the round.  The channel and its endpoints
  5253	        # read the absolute deadline of the round from it and they never
  5254	        # keep a budget of their own.
  5255	        self._context = context
  5256	        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._drain, daemon=True,
  5257	                                        name='lvw-native-custody-channel')
  5258	        # The channel registers itself with the custody of the launcher
  5259	        # **before** it builds anything: a channel whose construction
  5260	        # failed half way - an endpoint that could not be built, a
  5261	        # reader thread that did not start - may not leave an endpoint
  5262	        # and a thread without an owner.  The outer owner of the launch
  5263	        # finds the partial channel through this registration and it
  5264	        # never closes the files of the process by itself.
  5265	        self._custody = custody
  5266	        if custody is not None:
  5267	            custody.retain('channel', self, 'constructing')
  5268	        try:
  5269	            self._reader = read_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
  5270	                reader, name + '-read', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
  5271	            # The parameter of the constructor is ``write_endpoint`` and
  5272	            # the attribute of the channel is ``_writer``.  The two names
  5273	            # are never mixed: a name that does not exist raises a
  5274	            # ``NameError`` and every construction of a channel of this
  5275	            # package fails with it.
  5276	            self._writer = write_endpoint or _make_endpoint(
  5277	                writer, name + '-write', clock=clock, api=api, context=context)
  5278	            self._thread.start()
  5279	        except BaseException:
  5280	            # The construction of the channel did not return: the
  5281	            # channel keeps its owner in the custody of the launcher and
  5282	            # it is never forgotten.
  5283	            raise
  5284	
  5285	    def bind_context(self, context):
  5286	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to the channel.
  5287	
  5288	        The context is bound to the channel **and** to its two
  5289	        endpoints: every layer of the round reads the same absolute
  5290	        deadline and none of them keeps a deadline of its own.
  5291	        """
  5292	        if context is not None:
  5293	            self._context = context
  5294	            for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
  5295	                binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_context', None)
  5296	                if callable(binder):
  5297	                    binder(context)
  5298	        return self._context
  5299	
  5300	    def bind_deadline(self, deadline):
  5301	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the channel.
  5302	
  5303	        The deadline is bound to the channel **and** to its two
  5304	        endpoints.  A binding that happens after a read started still
  5305	        tightens that read: the deadline is a property of the endpoint
  5306	        and not a local argument of one call.
  5307	        """
  5308	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  5309	        if value is None:
  5310	            return self._deadline
  5311	        if self._deadline is not None and value > self._deadline:
  5312	            return self._deadline
  5313	        self._deadline = value
  5314	        for endpoint in (self._reader, self._writer):
  5315	            binder = getattr(endpoint, 'bind_deadline', None)
  5316	            if callable(binder):
  5317	                try:
  5318	                    binder(value)
  5319	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5320	                    pass
  5321	        return value
  5322	
  5323	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  5324	        """The ONE deadline that a call of this channel really uses.
  5325	
  5326	        The deadline of the call, the deadline that is bound to the
  5327	        channel, the absolute deadline of the round and the **published**
  5328	        cleanup deadline of the round are one value: the earliest of the
  5329	        four.
  5330	
  5331	        The published cleanup deadline is part of it.  A round that
  5332	        entered its cleanup publishes one deadline, and the transport of
  5333	        the round - the write of the terminal reply and the close of the
  5334	        channel - is bound by it: a transport that still waited with the
  5335	        long lease of the round would outlive the only cleanup budget
  5336	        of the round.
  5337	        """
  5338	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  5339	        bound = self._deadline
  5340	        if bound is not None and (value is None or bound < value):
  5341	            value = bound
  5342	        context = self._context
  5343	        if context is not None:
  5344	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  5345	            if absolute is not None and (value is None or absolute < value):
  5346	                value = absolute
  5347	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  5348	            if cleanup is not None and (value is None or cleanup < value):
  5349	                value = cleanup
  5350	        return value
  5351	
  5352	    def _remaining(self, deadline=None):
  5353	        """The seconds that are left until the deadline of the channel."""
  5354	        value = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  5355	        if value is None:
  5356	            return None
  5357	        return max(0.0, value - (self._clock or time.monotonic)())
  5358	
  5359	    def _drain(self):
  5360	        try:
  5361	            while True:
  5362	                block = self._reader.read(self._deadline, self._cancel)
  5363	                if not block:
  5364	                    break
  5365	                buffer = self._buffer + block
  5366	                self._buffer = b''
  5367	                while True:
  5368	                    index = buffer.find(b'\n')
  5369	                    if index < 0:
  5370	                        break
  5371	                    raw = buffer[:index + 1]
  5372	                    buffer = buffer[index + 1:]
  5373	                    if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
  5374	                        self._record_overflow('FRAME_TOO_LARGE',
  5375	                                              {'frame_bytes': len(raw),
  5376	                                               'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
  5377	                        return
  5378	                    self._count += 1
  5379	                    if self._count > self._max_frames:
  5380	                        self._record_overflow('TOO_MANY_FRAMES',
  5381	                                              {'frames': self._count,
  5382	                                               'limit': self._max_frames})
  5383	                        return
  5384	                    while True:
  5385	                        self._check_wait()
  5386	                        try:
  5387	                            self._queue.put(raw, timeout=self._put_wait())
  5388	                            break
  5389	                        except queue.Full:
  5390	                            if self._eof:
  5391	                                return
  5392	                            continue
  5393	                # The remainder of the block has no newline.  The buffer
  5394	                # of the channel is bounded: it is checked on **every**
  5395	                # append and an oversize buffer is refused at once.  One
  5396	                # byte beyond the limit is kept, so that the overflow is
  5397	                # detected and not silently trimmed.
  5398	                if len(buffer) > self._max_frame_bytes:
  5399	                    self._buffer = buffer[:self._max_frame_bytes + 1]
  5400	                    self._record_overflow('BUFFER_TOO_LARGE',
  5401	                                          {'buffer_bytes': len(self._buffer),
  5402	                                           'limit': self._max_frame_bytes})
  5403	                    return
  5404	                self._buffer = buffer
  5405	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5406	            self._record_reader_error(error)
  5407	        finally:
  5408	            self._eof = True
  5409	            if self._buffer and self._error is None:
  5410	                # The stream ended in the middle of a frame.  An
  5411	                # incomplete frame is a named failure of the channel and
  5412	                # it is never dropped silently.
  5413	                self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_FRAME'
  5414	
  5415	    def _record_overflow(self, reason, facts):
  5416	        """Record the overflow of the channel at the place where it happened.
  5417	
  5418	        The overflow is a **named** failure of the channel and it is
  5419	        recorded **here**, where it really happened.  A flag that only
  5420	        the internals of the channel know would leave the close of the
  5421	        channel with no error at all: the reader of a channel that
  5422	        overflowed would look like a reader that ended normally and the
  5423	        round would release its resources on a fact that never happened.
  5424	
  5425	        The error of the channel is **sticky**: the first named failure
  5426	        of the reader stays the error of the channel and the overflow
  5427	        never replaces it.
  5428	        """
  5429	        self._overflow = True
  5430	        self._overflow_facts = dict(facts, reason=reason)
  5431	        if self._error is None:
  5432	            self._error = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW'
  5433	
  5434	    def _record_reader_error(self, error):
  5435	        """Record the error of the reader of this channel.
  5436	
  5437	        A cancellation that **this** channel initiated with **this**
  5438	        close is not a failure of the reader: it is a named fact of the
  5439	        close.  It is kept with its code, with its source and with its
  5440	        order for the evidence, and it never replaces the error of the
  5441	        reader and it never clears a historical error of the reader.
  5442	
  5443	        Every other error keeps the original error of the reader and the
  5444	        round stays unknown: an ordinary failure of the read, a timeout,
  5445	        an overflow, a partial frame, an error that was already there
  5446	        before the close and a cancellation whose owner is unknown or
  5447	        is not the close of this channel.
  5448	        """
  5449	        source = getattr(error, 'lvw_cancel_source', None)
  5450	        if source is not None and source is self._close_source:
  5451	            # This cancellation was initiated by the close of this
  5452	            # channel: it is a named fact of the close and it is not a
  5453	            # failure of the reader.  The historical error of the reader
  5454	            # is never cleared and it is never replaced by this fact.
  5455	            self._close_cancellation = {
  5456	                'code': getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__,
  5457	                'source': getattr(source, 'owner', 'CHANNEL_CLOSE'),
  5458	                'sequence': getattr(source, 'sequence', None),
  5459	                'error_type': type(error).__name__,
  5460	            }
  5461	            return
  5462	        parallel = getattr(error, 'lvw_parallel_cancellation', None)
  5463	        if parallel is not None:
  5464	            # The cancellation that arrived at the same moment as the
  5465	            # failure of the reader is a **parallel** fact: it is named
  5466	            # next to the failure and it never replaces it.
  5467	            self._parallel_cancellation = dict(parallel)
  5468	        self._error = getattr(error, 'code', None) or type(error).__name__
  5469	
  5470	    def _check_wait(self):
  5471	        """The cancel flag and the deadline of the wait of the reader."""
  5472	        if self._cancel.is_set():
  5473	            # The error carries the owner of the flag: the reader of
  5474	            # the channel and the close of the channel agree on the
  5475	            # origin of the cancellation and the evidence can prove it.
  5476	            raise _cancel_source_error(BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_CANCELLED'),
  5477	                                       self._cancel)
  5478	        remaining = self._remaining()
  5479	        if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
  5480	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT')
  5481	
  5482	    def _put_wait(self):
  5483	        """The seconds that the next put of the reader may ask for.
  5484	
  5485	        The put is the bounded wait of the package, cropped to the
  5486	        time that is really left until the **current** effective
  5487	        deadline of the round.  There is no positive floor beyond it:
  5488	        an expired round is refused by the check that precedes the put
  5489	        and the put itself never waits longer than the round allows.
  5490	        """
  5491	        remaining = self._remaining()
  5492	        if remaining is None:
  5493	            return CHANNEL_QUEUE_PUT_SECONDS
  5494	        return max(0.0, min(CHANNEL_QUEUE_PUT_SECONDS, remaining))
  5495	
  5496	    def read(self, timeout):
  5497	        """One raw frame, ``None`` when nothing arrived, ``b''`` at the end.
  5498	
  5499	        The wait of the reader is cropped to the **current** effective
  5500	        deadline of the round: the bound of the channel, the absolute
  5501	        deadline of the round and the **published** cleanup deadline of
  5502	        the round are read **again** at the moment of the read.  There is
  5503	        **no positive floor** beyond them: a round whose bound arrived
  5504	        asks for nothing at all and the read becomes a non blocking
  5505	        inspection.
  5506	
  5507	        The crop applies to **every** reader of the channel: the loop of
  5508	        the broker and the pump of the parent read the same channel and
  5509	        neither of them may wait with a lease of its own.
  5510	        """
  5511	        if self._overflow:
  5512	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW')
  5513	        limit = max(0.0, timeout)
  5514	        remaining = self._remaining()
  5515	        if remaining is not None:
  5516	            if remaining <= 0:
  5517	                # The bound of the round arrived: the read asks for
  5518	                # nothing and it never opens a wait of its own.
  5519	                limit = 0.0
  5520	            elif remaining < limit:
  5521	                limit = remaining
  5522	        try:
  5523	            return self._queue.get(timeout=limit)
  5524	        except queue.Empty:
  5525	            if self._error:
  5526	                raise BrokerError(self._error)
  5527	            if self._eof:
  5528	                return b''
  5529	            return None
  5530	
  5531	    def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
  5532	        """Write one frame, bounded by the deadline of the round.
  5533	
  5534	        The number of the bytes that really went into the pipe is
  5535	        checked: a partial write is never reported as a success.
  5536	        """
  5537	        if len(raw) > self._max_frame_bytes:
  5538	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_FRAME_TOO_LARGE')
  5539	        target = self._effective_deadline(deadline)
  5540	        written = self._writer.write(raw, target, self._cancel)
  5541	        if written is not None and written != len(raw):
  5542	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_CHANNEL_WRITE_INCOMPLETE',
  5543	                              'written=%s:expected=%d' % (written, len(raw)))
  5544	        return written
  5545	
  5546	    def close(self):
  5547	        """Close the channel and report whether the reader really stopped.
  5548	
  5549	        The reader thread is joined with a bounded wait.  A reader that
  5550	        did not stop is reported as such: a daemon thread is not a
  5551	        finished thread, so the fact that the reader is still alive is
  5552	        kept visible instead of being hidden behind the close.  The read
  5553	        end is closed by its owner only when the reader really stopped.
  5554	
  5555	        The facts of the close carry the error of the reader, the number
  5556	        of the workers that the endpoints retained and the fact that the
  5557	        original handles of those workers were released: a reader that
  5558	        ended with an error is not a successful end.
  5559	
  5560	        The close may be **repeated**: a close that did not confirm its
  5561	        ends may try them again, and a close of an endpoint that is
  5562	        already closed keeps the fact that it is closed.
  5563	
  5564	        The error of the reader of the channel is **sticky** and it is
  5565	        never cleared by a later close of the channel: a close that
  5566	        returned is not a release and the error of the reader stays.
  5567	
  5568	        The close **initiates** the cancellation of the reader and it
  5569	        names itself as the owner of that cancellation before the flag
  5570	        is set.  A cancellation that this close really initiated is a
  5571	        named fact of the close and it is not a failure of the reader; a
  5572	        cancellation of another owner, a failure of the read that
  5573	        happened before the close and every other error of the reader
  5574	        keep the round unknown.
  5575	        """
  5576	        facts = {}
  5577	        # The identity of this close is published **before** the
  5578	        # cancellation is initiated and the flag registers the owner in
  5579	        # the same atomic step as the setting of the flag.
  5580	        self._close_sequence += 1
  5581	        source = _CloseSource(self._close_sequence)
  5582	        self._close_source = source
  5583	        self._cancel.set(source)
  5584	        try:
  5585	            write_facts = self._writer.close()
  5586	            facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(write_facts.get('closed'))
  5587	            facts['write_retained_workers'] = int(
  5588	                write_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
  5589	            facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5590	                write_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5591	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5592	            facts['write_end_closed'] = False
  5593	            facts['write_retained_workers'] = 0
  5594	            facts['write_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
  5595	        remaining = self._remaining()
  5596	        join_seconds = CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS if remaining is None else remaining
  5597	        start_unconfirmed = False
  5598	        try:
  5599	            self._thread.join(join_seconds)
  5600	        except RuntimeError:
  5601	            # The join could not prove the end of the reader: the start
  5602	            # of the thread of the reader was **interrupted** or it was
  5603	            # never confirmed.  A start that did not return may still
  5604	            # come up and a late reader may still read from the read
  5605	            # end of the channel.  The state of the reader therefore
  5606	            # stays **unknown**: the read end is never closed underneath
  5607	            # a possibly late target and the round keeps the fact.
  5608	            self.reader_alive = True
  5609	            self.reader_finished = False
  5610	            start_unconfirmed = True
  5611	        else:
  5612	            self.reader_alive = self._thread.is_alive()
  5613	            self.reader_finished = not self.reader_alive
  5614	        facts['reader_finished'] = self.reader_finished
  5615	        facts['reader_error'] = self._error
  5616	        # The start of the reader that was never confirmed is a named
  5617	        # fact of the close: the state of the reader is unknown and it
  5618	        # is never read as a finished reader.
  5619	        facts['reader_start_unconfirmed'] = start_unconfirmed
  5620	        # The facts of the overflow are published with the close: the
  5621	        # overflow is a named failure of the channel and it is never
  5622	        # only an internal flag.
  5623	        facts['overflow'] = bool(self._overflow)
  5624	        facts['overflow_facts'] = dict(self._overflow_facts)
  5625	        # The parallel fact of the cancellation of the close: it never
  5626	        # replaces the error of the reader.
  5627	        facts['reader_parallel_cancellation'] = (
  5628	            dict(self._parallel_cancellation)
  5629	            if self._parallel_cancellation else None)
  5630	        # The named fact of the cancellation that this close really
  5631	        # initiated.  It is **not** the error of the reader and it does
  5632	        # not prove that the reader, the thread, the worker or the
  5633	        # handle of the round were released: the strict facts of the
  5634	        # close above are the ones that prove it.
  5635	        facts['close_cancellation'] = (dict(self._close_cancellation)
  5636	                                       if self._close_cancellation else None)
  5637	        if self.reader_alive:
  5638	            # The reader is still inside the pipe: the read end is not
  5639	            # closed under it.  It stays owned by the channel and the
  5640	            # round keeps the fact.  The facts of the reader endpoint are
  5641	            # the real ones and they are never replaced by a zero.
  5642	            endpoint_facts = getattr(self._reader, 'facts', None)
  5643	            endpoint_facts = endpoint_facts if isinstance(endpoint_facts, dict) else {}
  5644	            facts['read_end_closed'] = False
  5645	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
  5646	                endpoint_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
  5647	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5648	                endpoint_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5649	            facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5650	                facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
  5651	                and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5652	            facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
  5653	                                                  + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
  5654	            return facts
  5655	        try:
  5656	            read_facts = self._reader.close()
  5657	            facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(read_facts.get('closed'))
  5658	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = int(
  5659	                read_facts.get('retained_workers') or 0)
  5660	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5661	                read_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5662	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5663	            facts['read_end_closed'] = False
  5664	            facts['read_retained_workers'] = 0
  5665	            facts['read_worker_thread_handle_released'] = False
  5666	        facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  5667	            facts.get('write_worker_thread_handle_released')
  5668	            and facts.get('read_worker_thread_handle_released'))
  5669	        facts['channel_retained_workers'] = (int(facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
  5670	                                              + int(facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
  5671	        return facts
  5672	
  5673	    @property
  5674	    def eof(self):
  5675	        return self._eof
  5676	
  5677	    @property
  5678	    def overflow(self):
  5679	        return self._overflow
  5680	
  5681	    @property
  5682	    def frames(self):
  5683	        return self._count
  5684	
  5685	
  5686	# -- the bounded command loop ----------------------------------------------
  5687	
  5688	class _StartupOwner:
  5689	    """The bounded owner of one startup step of the round.
  5690	
  5691	    A startup step may block: the creation of a job, the creation of a
  5692	    process and the assignment of a process may all block inside the
  5693	    operating system.  Such a step may not run unbounded inside the one
  5694	    supervision thread of the round.
  5695	
  5696	    The owner therefore runs the step in its own thread and hands the
  5697	    result over through a **bounded** channel of one slot.  The
  5698	    supervisor waits for the result until the absolute deadline of the
  5699	    round.  A step that did not finish in time keeps the round unknown,
  5700	    keeps the thread and its owner, and a result that arrives late may
  5701	    only clean up: it may never continue the startup of the round.
  5702	    """
  5703	
  5704	    def __init__(self, *, name, clock, deadline, context=None):
  5705	        self.name = name
  5706	        self._clock = clock
  5707	        self._deadline = deadline
  5708	        # The ONE context of the round.  The owner reads the **current**
  5709	        # absolute deadline and the **published** cleanup deadline of
  5710	        # the round from it and it never keeps a copy of a deadline of
  5711	        # its own.
  5712	        self._context = context
  5713	        self._results = queue.Queue(maxsize=STARTUP_INFLIGHT_SLOTS)
  5714	        self._thread = None
  5715	        self._error = None
  5716	        self._value = None
  5717	        self._finished = threading.Event()
  5718	        self._expired = False
  5719	        # Whether the start of the thread of this owner **returned**.
  5720	        # A start that did not return may still come up: the owner keeps
  5721	        # its thread and the round keeps the fact.  The flag is never
  5722	        # the authority of the question: the facts of the thread itself
  5723	        # are.
  5724	        self._started = False
  5725	
  5726	    def start(self, action):
  5727	        """Run one startup step in the thread of this owner.
  5728	
  5729	        The start of the thread is **one** step and it is recorded: a
  5730	        start that did not return keeps its owner and the thread of the
  5731	        step may still come up.  The owner is never released because
  5732	        the start failed.
  5733	
  5734	        A start that raised **after** the thread really came up leaves
  5735	        the flag of the owner unset, and the facts of the thread are
  5736	        consulted instead: the thread of the step is a thread of the
  5737	        round and the owner may join it.
  5738	        """
  5739	        self._thread = threading.Thread(
  5740	            target=self._run, args=(action,), daemon=True,
  5741	            name='lvw-native-custody-startup-' + self.name)
  5742	        try:
  5743	            self._thread.start()
  5744	        except BaseException:
  5745	            # The start did not return: the thread may really have come
  5746	            # up and the facts of the thread decide.
  5747	            self._started = self._thread_facts_started()
  5748	            raise
  5749	        self._started = True
  5750	        return self
  5751	
  5752	    def _thread_facts_started(self):
  5753	        """Whether the original facts of the thread prove that it started.
  5754	
  5755	        The private flag of the owner is **not** the authority: it is
  5756	        written after the start returned and a start that raised after
  5757	        the thread really came up leaves it unset.  The identity of the
  5758	        thread and its liveness are the facts that decide whether a
  5759	        real join may be attempted.
  5760	        """
  5761	        thread = self._thread
  5762	        if thread is None:
  5763	            return False
  5764	        try:
  5765	            if getattr(thread, 'ident', None) is not None:
  5766	                return True
  5767	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5768	            return False
  5769	        try:
  5770	            return bool(thread.is_alive())
  5771	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5772	            return False
  5773	
  5774	    def _remaining(self):
  5775	        """The seconds that are left until the current bound of the step.
  5776	
  5777	        The bound is the **current** effective deadline of the original
  5778	        context of the round - its absolute deadline and the published
  5779	        cleanup deadline - together with the deadline that the owner
  5780	        received.  The value is recomputed on every call: a deadline
  5781	        that was published while the step was running shortens the wait
  5782	        that is running.
  5783	        """
  5784	        candidates = []
  5785	        context = self._context
  5786	        if context is not None:
  5787	            absolute = context.absolute_deadline
  5788	            if absolute is not None:
  5789	                candidates.append(absolute)
  5790	            cleanup = context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  5791	            if cleanup is not None:
  5792	                candidates.append(cleanup)
  5793	        if self._deadline is not None:
  5794	            candidates.append(self._deadline)
  5795	        if not candidates:
  5796	            return None
  5797	        return max(0.0, min(candidates) - self._clock())
  5798	
  5799	    def _cancelled(self):
  5800	        """Whether the original context of the round asked for the stop."""
  5801	        context = self._context
  5802	        return context is not None and context.cancel.is_set()
  5803	
  5804	    def _run(self, action):
  5805	        try:
  5806	            value = action()
  5807	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  5808	            self._error = error
  5809	            outcome = ('error', error)
  5810	        else:
  5811	            self._value = value
  5812	            outcome = ('value', value)
  5813	        # The **attempt** of the step ended.  The event of the attempt is
  5814	        # published here and it is never a proof that the thread of the
  5815	        # step ended: the thread still has to publish its result and to
  5816	        # leave.
  5817	        self._finished.set()
  5818	        self._put(outcome)
  5819	
  5820	    def _put(self, item):
  5821	        try:
  5822	            self._results.put_nowait(item)
  5823	        except queue.Full:
  5824	            # The bounded channel is full: the result is dropped and the
  5825	            # owner keeps the error.  The channel never grows.
  5826	            pass
  5827	
  5828	    def await_result(self):
  5829	        """Wait for the result of the step, bounded by the deadline.
  5830	
  5831	        The result that is already published is **never** replaced by a
  5832	        refusal of the deadline: the original failure of the step is
  5833	        the error that leaves.
  5834	
  5835	        The wait of the next slice is cropped to the time that is
  5836	        really left until the **current** effective deadline of the
  5837	        original context of the round and there is no positive floor
  5838	        beyond it: an expired round refuses at once.
  5839	        """
  5840	        while True:
  5841	            try:
  5842	                kind, value = self._results.get_nowait()
  5843	            except queue.Empty:
  5844	                kind = None
  5845	                value = None
  5846	            if kind is not None:
  5847	                if kind == 'error':
  5848	                    raise value
  5849	                return value
  5850	            remaining = self._remaining()
  5851	            if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
  5852	                self._expired = True
  5853	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', self.name)
  5854	            if self._cancelled():
  5855	                raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', self.name)
  5856	            wait = POLL_SLICE_SECONDS
  5857	            if remaining is not None:
  5858	                wait = max(0.0, min(wait, remaining))
  5859	            try:
  5860	                kind, value = self._results.get(timeout=wait)
  5861	            except queue.Empty:
  5862	                continue
  5863	            if kind == 'error':
  5864	                raise value
  5865	            return value
  5866	
  5867	    def settle(self, seconds):
  5868	        """Wait, bounded, for the **thread** of this owner to end.
  5869	
  5870	        The wait is bounded by the shared cleanup deadline of the round
  5871	        and it never adds a settle budget of its own.
  5872	
  5873	        The end of the **attempt** of the step and the end of the
  5874	        **thread** that performs it are two different facts.  The event
  5875	        of the attempt is published before the result and before the
  5876	        thread leaves, and it is therefore never a proof that the
  5877	        thread ended.  The settle asks the thread itself: a thread that
  5878	        is still running keeps its owner and the round stays unknown.
  5879	
  5880	        A **start that did not return** is unknown only when the facts
  5881	        of the thread do not prove that it came up.  A thread that
  5882	        really started is joined for real, even when the start raised
  5883	        afterwards: the owner of the step coordinates the thread that it
  5884	        really has and it never releases it without a proof.
  5885	        """
  5886	        if self._thread is None:
  5887	            return True
  5888	        if not self._started and not self._thread_facts_started():
  5889	            # The start of the thread did not return and the thread
  5890	            # really did not come up: it may still come up and the owner
  5891	            # is never released.
  5892	            return False
  5893	        try:
  5894	            self._thread.join(max(0.0, seconds))
  5895	        except RuntimeError:
  5896	            # The thread was never started: it is not a thread of the
  5897	            # round that ended.
  5898	            return False
  5899	        return not self._thread.is_alive()
  5900	
  5901	    @property
  5902	    def expired(self):
  5903	        """Whether the step of this owner missed the deadline."""
  5904	        return self._expired
  5905	
  5906	    @property
  5907	    def in_flight(self):
  5908	        """Whether the step of this owner is still running."""
  5909	        return self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive()
  5910	
  5911	
  5912	class BrokerLoop:
  5913	    """The bounded command loop of the broker.
  5914	
  5915	    The loop owns the absolute deadline of the round.  It is fixed once,
  5916	    from the lease of the plan, and it is never moved: neither a status
  5917	    request nor a heartbeat may extend it.
  5918	
  5919	    The round is stopped when the parent asks for it, when the channel
  5920	    reaches its end, when a bound of the channel or of the text of the
  5921	    children is exceeded or when the absolute deadline arrives.  A stop
  5922	    that was not requested by the parent keeps the evidence of the round
  5923	    unknown: the round did not end in a proven release.
  5924	
  5925	    The startup of the round is supervised.  The arm and the launch are
  5926	    **bounded startup steps** with their own owner: a step that blocks
  5927	    may not hold the supervision of the round and a step that returns
  5928	    after the absolute deadline may only clean up.
  5929	    """
  5930	
  5931	    def __init__(self, *, core, transport, plan, nonce, clock=None, wall=None,
  5932	                 max_frames=MAX_FRAMES):
  5933	        self.core = core
  5934	        self.transport = transport
  5935	        self.plan = plan
  5936	        self.nonce = nonce
  5937	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  5938	        self._wall = wall or time.time
  5939	        self._max_frames = max_frames
  5940	        self._sequence = 0
  5941	        self._last_sequence = None
  5942	        self._frames = 0
  5943	        self._sent_frames = 0
  5944	        self._deadline_wall = None
  5945	        self._deadline_mono = None
  5946	        self._last_frame_wall = None
  5947	        self._last_alive_wall = None
  5948	        self._stopped = False
  5949	        # The ONE context of the round.  The loop holds the very same
  5950	        # object as the core: the deadlines, the cancel and the unknown
  5951	        # of the round are one state and not one number per layer.
  5952	        self.context = core.context
  5953	        # The owner of the startup step that is in flight.  A step that
  5954	        # did not end keeps the round unknown and it keeps its owner.
  5955	        self._startup_owner = None
  5956	        self._startup_in_flight = None
  5957	
  5958	    # -- the deadline ------------------------------------------------------
  5959	    def fix_deadline(self):
  5960	        """Fix the absolute deadline of the round and return it.
  5961	
  5962	        The deadline is fixed **before** the transport of the round
  5963	        exists: the reader of the channel and the workers of the
  5964	        endpoints never start without a deadline and the child has no
  5965	        unbound window.
  5966	        """
  5967	        return self._fix_deadline()
  5968	
  5969	    def bind_transport(self, transport):
  5970	        """Bind the transport of the round to this loop.
  5971	
  5972	        The transport is bound after the deadline of the round was
  5973	        fixed: the transport of the round is never created without the
  5974	        deadline that bounds it.
  5975	        """
  5976	        self.transport = transport
  5977	        return transport
  5978	
  5979	    def _fix_deadline(self):
  5980	        """Fix the absolute deadline of the round, once, before anything.
  5981	
  5982	        The deadline is fixed before the job of the round is created,
  5983	        before the first reply is written and before the first role is
  5984	        launched.  It is the earlier of the lease of the plan and the
  5985	        runtime of the plan and it is never moved afterwards.
  5986	        """
  5987	        if self._deadline_mono is not None:
  5988	            return self._deadline_mono
  5989	        wall_now = self._wall()
  5990	        self._deadline_wall = min(self.plan.lease_expires_at,
  5991	                                  wall_now + self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
  5992	        self._deadline_mono = self._clock() + max(0.0, self._deadline_wall - wall_now)
  5993	        return self._deadline_mono
  5994	
  5995	    def remaining_seconds(self):
  5996	        if self._deadline_mono is None:
  5997	            return float(self.plan.max_runtime_seconds)
  5998	        return max(0.0, self._deadline_mono - self._clock())
  5999	
  6000	    # -- the replies -------------------------------------------------------
  6001	    def _check_frame_budget(self, reserved=RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES):
  6002	        """The frame budget of the channel, in both directions.
  6003	
  6004	        The budget of the round is shared by the frames that the broker
  6005	        sends and by the frames that it receives: the round of six hours
  6006	        with its heartbeat stays inside the budget of the channel.  The
  6007	        frames of the terminal exchange are reserved and the budget is
  6008	        never widened and never renewed.
  6009	
  6010	        The check is the **total** of both directions.  A single
  6011	        direction is never a substitute for the total: a channel that
  6012	        received a thousand frames and sent none is not inside the
  6013	        budget when the budget is a thousand and twenty four frames.
  6014	        """
