  6015	        if self._frames + self._sent_frames + reserved + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
  6016	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED',
  6017	                              'sent=%d:received=%d' % (self._sent_frames,
  6018	                                                       self._frames))
  6019	
  6020	    def _reply(self, kind, state, detail, *, terminal=False):
  6021	        self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
  6022	        self._sent_frames += 1
  6023	        self._sequence += 1
  6024	        frame = {'schema': CHANNEL_SCHEMA, 'kind': kind, 'run_id': self.plan.run_id,
  6025	                 'candidate_sha': self.plan.candidate_sha,
  6026	                 'authorization_sha256': self.plan.authorization_sha256,
  6027	                 'nonce': self.nonce, 'sequence': self._sequence,
  6028	                 'state': state, 'detail': detail}
  6029	        raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
  6030	                         separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
  6031	        if len(raw) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
  6032	            frame['detail'] = {'truncated': True}
  6033	            raw = json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
  6034	                             separators=(',', ':'), allow_nan=False).encode('utf-8')
  6035	        # The deadline of the round is already bound to the transport, so
  6036	        # the write of the frame is bounded by it.  The transport is
  6037	        # never called with an argument that it does not know.
  6038	        self.transport.write(raw + b'\n')
  6039	
  6040	    # -- the supervised startup --------------------------------------------
  6041	    def _guarded_startup(self, action, stage):
  6042	        """One startup step, guarded by the shared deadline.
  6043	
  6044	        The shared budget is checked **before** the step and **after**
  6045	        it returned.  A step that starts after the deadline of the round
  6046	        arrived never runs at all, and a step that finished after the
  6047	        deadline may only clean up: it may never continue the startup of
  6048	        the round and it may never write a successful reply.
  6049	
  6050	        The supervision publishes the cancel of the round when the
  6051	        deadline arrived: the core and every later step of the startup
  6052	        see it and they stop.
  6053	        """
  6054	        def guarded():
  6055	            self._check_startup_budget(stage)
  6056	            action()
  6057	            self._check_startup_budget(stage)
  6058	            return None
  6059	        return guarded
  6060	
  6061	    def _check_startup_budget(self, stage):
  6062	        """Check the shared budget of the startup of the round."""
  6063	        try:
  6064	            self.core.check_startup_budget(stage)
  6065	        except BrokerError:
  6066	            # The supervision publishes the cancel of the round: the
  6067	            # core and every later step of the startup see it.
  6068	            self.context.request_cancel()
  6069	            self._abort_started_round()
  6070	            raise
  6071	
  6072	    def _abort_started_round(self):
  6073	        """Release what the round acquired, bounded by the shared budget."""
  6074	        try:
  6075	            self.core.stop('STARTUP_AFTER_DEADLINE', force_unknown=True)
  6076	        except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6077	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'startup_abort',
  6078	                                       'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  6079	
  6080	    def _run_startup(self, stage, action):
  6081	        """Run one startup step under the absolute deadline of the round.
  6082	
  6083	        **Every** exit of this method waits for the **real** exit of the
  6084	        thread of the step: the success, the timeout, the ordinary
  6085	        failure, the cancellation and the ``SystemExit``.  The attempt
  6086	        of the step and the thread that performs it are two different
  6087	        facts and the owner of the step is released only after the real
  6088	        exit of its thread.
  6089	
  6090	        A thread that did not exit keeps its owner: it stays in the
  6091	        owners that are in flight and the round keeps the fact.  It is
  6092	        never released because the attempt ended and it is never given a
  6093	        new budget.
  6094	        """
  6095	        owner = _StartupOwner(name=stage, clock=self._clock,
  6096	                              deadline=self._deadline_mono,
  6097	                              context=self.context)
  6098	        self._startup_owner = owner
  6099	        self.context.register_inflight(owner)
  6100	        try:
  6101	            # The start of the step is **inside** the ownership of the
  6102	            # owner and inside the ``finally`` below: a start that did
  6103	            # not return is coordinated like every other exit and the
  6104	            # owner is never left in the flight without a decision.
  6105	            owner.start(self._guarded_startup(action, stage))
  6106	            value = owner.await_result()
  6107	        except BrokerError as error:
  6108	            if error.code not in ('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED',
  6109	                                  'BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED'):
  6110	                # An ordinary failure of the step: the thread of the
  6111	                # step is still coordinated in the finally below and the
  6112	                # original failure is the one that leaves this method.
  6113	                raise
  6114	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage, 'code': error.code})
  6115	            # The step did not finish before the deadline.  The owner
  6116	            # keeps its thread: a late result may only clean up.
  6117	            raise
  6118	        finally:
  6119	            # The budget of the settle is **recomputed after the await**
  6120	            # and immediately before the join: a deadline that was
  6121	            # published while the step was running still shortens the
  6122	            # wait and a settle budget that was copied before the await
  6123	            # is never used.  There is no positive floor beyond the
  6124	            # time that is really left.
  6125	            settle_budget = self._startup_settle_seconds()
  6126	            # The real exit of the thread is asked on **every** exit.
  6127	            try:
  6128	                settled = owner.settle(settle_budget)
  6129	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6130	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
  6131	                                           'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  6132	                settled = False
  6133	            if not settled:
  6134	                self._startup_in_flight = owner
  6135	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': stage,
  6136	                                           'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
  6137	            else:
  6138	                self.context.release_inflight(owner)
  6139	        return value
  6140	
  6141	    def _startup_settle_seconds(self):
  6142	        """The bounded wait for the real exit of a startup thread.
  6143	
  6144	        The wait is bounded by the **remaining** of the absolute
  6145	        deadline of the round and by the settle bound of the package.
  6146	        It never publishes the cleanup deadline of the round - the
  6147	        cleanup of a round that did not fail keeps its whole budget -
  6148	        and it never opens a budget of its own.
  6149	
  6150	        The remaining is recomputed on every call and it is never
  6151	        rounded **up**: a settle budget that is larger than the time
  6152	        that is really left would wait past the deadline of the round.
  6153	        The published cleanup deadline of the round shortens it when the
  6154	        round already entered its cleanup.
  6155	        """
  6156	        remaining = self.context.remaining_absolute()
  6157	        cleanup = self.context.cleanup_deadline_value()
  6158	        if cleanup is not None:
  6159	            left = max(0.0, cleanup - self._clock())
  6160	            remaining = left if remaining is None else min(remaining, left)
  6161	        if remaining is None:
  6162	            return STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS
  6163	        return max(0.0, min(remaining, STARTUP_SETTLE_SECONDS))
  6164	
  6165	    # -- the run -----------------------------------------------------------
  6166	    def run(self):
  6167	        """Run the round and always release what the round owns.
  6168	
  6169	        The loop has a ``finally`` that does not depend on the diagnosis
  6170	        of the round and not on the success of a write: a round that
  6171	        could not write its first reply still releases the job and the
  6172	        handles that it created.
  6173	        """
  6174	        self._fix_deadline()
  6175	        # The core and the loop read the same clock: the deadlines of
  6176	        # the round and the ONE cleanup deadline are in one domain.
  6177	        binder_clock = getattr(self.core, 'bind_clock', None)
  6178	        if callable(binder_clock):
  6179	            binder_clock(self._clock)
  6180	        self.core.bind_absolute_deadline(self._deadline_mono)
  6181	        binder = getattr(self.transport, 'bind_deadline', None)
  6182	        if callable(binder):
  6183	            # The transport of the channel is bound to the deadline of
  6184	            # the round before the job of the round exists: a read or a
  6185	            # write of the channel can never outlive the round.
  6186	            binder(self._deadline_mono)
  6187	        try:
  6188	            try:
  6189	                self._run_startup('ARM', self.core.arm)
  6190	            except BrokerError as error:
  6191	                self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
  6192	                            terminal=True)
  6193	                self._write_receipt('ARM_FAILED')
  6194	                return 3
  6195	            self._reply('READY', self.core.state, {'broker': self.core.broker_identity})
  6196	            try:
  6197	                self._run_startup('LAUNCH', self.core.launch)
  6198	            except BrokerError as error:
  6199	                self._reply('REFUSED', self.core.state, {'code': error.code},
  6200	                            terminal=True)
  6201	                self._write_receipt('LAUNCH_FAILED')
  6202	                return 3
  6203	            self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
  6204	            exit_code = self._loop()
  6205	            self._write_receipt(self.core.stop_reason)
  6206	            return exit_code
  6207	        finally:
  6208	            self._ensure_stopped()
  6209	
  6210	    def _ensure_stopped(self):
  6211	        """Release the resources of the round, whatever happened before.
  6212	
  6213	        The release never raises: a failure of the release is kept by the
  6214	        core and it never replaces the first failure of the round.
  6215	        """
  6216	        if self._stopped:
  6217	            return
  6218	        self._stopped = True
  6219	        if self._startup_in_flight is not None:
  6220	            # A startup step is still running: the core belongs to the
  6221	            # in-flight owner and the round keeps the fact.  The cleanup
  6222	            # of the core would race with the step that is still inside
  6223	            # the operating system, so it is not started here.
  6224	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
  6225	                                       'code': 'BROKER_STARTUP_IN_FLIGHT_RETAINED'})
  6226	            self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  6227	            return
  6228	        try:
  6229	            self.core.stop(self.core.stop_reason or 'LOOP_EXIT', force_unknown=True)
  6230	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6231	            self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'loop_stop',
  6232	                                        'error_type': type(error).__name__})
  6233	            self.core.state = 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
  6234	
  6235	    def _context_remaining(self):
  6236	        """The seconds that are left until the current bound of the round.
  6237	
  6238	        The value is read **again** at every call: the absolute deadline
  6239	        of the round and the **published** cleanup deadline of the round
  6240	        are one minimum and the loop never keeps a copy of it.  ``None``
  6241	        means that the round did not bind a deadline at all and that the
  6242	        lease of the loop is the only bound.
  6243	        """
  6244	        context = self.context
  6245	        if context is None:
  6246	            return None
  6247	        value = context.effective_deadline()
  6248	        if value is None:
  6249	            return None
  6250	        clock = getattr(context, 'clock', None) or self._clock
  6251	        return max(0.0, value - clock())
  6252	
  6253	    def _loop(self):
  6254	        self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
  6255	        while True:
  6256	            now = self._clock()
  6257	            if now >= self._deadline_mono:
  6258	                return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
  6259	            if self.core.overflowed():
  6260	                return self._stop('BOUND_EXCEEDED', 4)
  6261	            # The read of the transport is bounded by the time that is
  6262	            # really left until the absolute deadline of the round.
  6263	            # There is **no positive floor** beyond it: a round whose
  6264	            # deadline arrived asks for nothing and the loop decides
  6265	            # the lease that arrived.
  6266	            remaining = self._deadline_mono - now
  6267	            if remaining <= 0:
  6268	                return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
  6269	            # The **current** minimum of the original context of the
  6270	            # round is part of the bound of the next read: the absolute
  6271	            # deadline and the **published** cleanup deadline of the
  6272	            # round are read again at the moment of the read and the
  6273	            # cached lease of the loop is never the only authority.
  6274	            current = self._context_remaining()
  6275	            if current is not None:
  6276	                if current <= 0:
  6277	                    return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
  6278	                if current < remaining:
  6279	                    remaining = current
  6280	            timeout = min(POLL_SECONDS, remaining)
  6281	            try:
  6282	                raw = self.transport.read(timeout)
  6283	            except BrokerError as error:
  6284	                if error.code == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT':
  6285	                    # The bound of the round arrived while the read was
  6286	                    # admitted: the lease of the round is what expired
  6287	                    # and it is not a failure of the channel.
  6288	                    return self._stop('LEASE_EXPIRED', 4)
  6289	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_ERROR', 4)
  6290	            if raw is None:
  6291	                # A missing heartbeat is a secondary signal.  The absolute
  6292	                # deadline stays the authority of the round.
  6293	                if self._wall() - self._last_frame_wall > HEARTBEAT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:
  6294	                    return self._stop('HEARTBEAT_MISSING', 4)
  6295	                continue
  6296	            if raw == b'':
  6297	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_EOF', 4)
  6298	            # The hard cap of the channel is checked before the frame is
  6299	            # decoded: a frame that does not fit at all is refused
  6300	            # without being accepted.
  6301	            if self._frames + self._sent_frames + 1 > MAX_FRAMES:
  6302	                return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
  6303	            try:
  6304	                frame = decode_command(raw, run_id=self.plan.run_id,
  6305	                                       candidate_sha=self.plan.candidate_sha,
  6306	                                       authorization_sha256=self.plan.authorization_sha256,
  6307	                                       nonce=self.nonce, last_sequence=self._last_sequence)
  6308	            except CustodyError as error:
  6309	                self.core.failures.append({'stage': 'channel', 'code': error.code})
  6310	                return self._stop('CHANNEL_REFUSED', 4)
  6311	            # The reservation of the terminal exchange is **consumed** by
  6312	            # the terminal frame itself.  A STOP that arrives near the
  6313	            # limit of the budget is a legal frame: it is never refused
  6314	            # because of the reservation that was kept for it.  A frame
  6315	            # that is not terminal never borrows the reservation and it
  6316	            # fails closed when the total does not fit.
  6317	            terminal = frame['command'] == 'STOP'
  6318	            try:
  6319	                self._check_frame_budget(0 if terminal else RESERVED_TERMINAL_FRAMES)
  6320	            except BrokerError:
  6321	                return self._stop('FRAME_BUDGET_EXCEEDED', 4)
  6322	            self._frames += 1
  6323	            if self._frames > self._max_frames:
  6324	                return self._stop('FRAME_COUNT_EXCEEDED', 4)
  6325	            # Only a frame that was decoded and that carries the identity
  6326	            # of the round refreshes the heartbeat of the round.  A frame
  6327	            # that was refused never does.
  6328	            self._last_frame_wall = self._wall()
  6329	            self._last_alive_wall = self._last_frame_wall
  6330	            self._last_sequence = frame['sequence']
  6331	            if frame['command'] == 'STATUS':
  6332	                self._reply('STATUS', self.core.state, self.core.summary())
  6333	                continue
  6334	            return self._stop('STOP_COMMAND', 0)
  6335	
  6336	    def _stop(self, reason, exit_code):
  6337	        unknown = reason != 'STOP_COMMAND'
  6338	        self.core.stop(reason, force_unknown=unknown)
  6339	        self._reply('STOPPED', self.core.state, self.core.summary(), terminal=True)
  6340	        return exit_code
  6341	
  6342	    def _write_receipt(self, reason):
  6343	        """Write the terminal receipt of the round into the run root.
  6344	
  6345	        The receipt is the evidence that survives a parent that died.  It
  6346	        is written once, it is bounded and it is never replaced by a
  6347	        successful claim.  A failure of the write never replaces the
  6348	        first failure of the round.
  6349	        """
  6350	        evidence = self.core.evidence()
  6351	        evidence['reason'] = reason
  6352	        evidence['channel'] = {'frames_received': self._frames,
  6353	                               'last_sequence_received': self._last_sequence,
  6354	                               'frame_limit': self._max_frames}
  6355	        evidence['deadline_wall'] = self._deadline_wall
  6356	        evidence['deadline_extended'] = False
  6357	        raw = json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
  6358	                         indent=2).encode('utf-8')
  6359	        if len(raw) > MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES:
  6360	            raw = json.dumps({'kind': 'lvw.s3-native-broker-receipt/1',
  6361	                              'reason': reason, 'truncated': True,
  6362	                              'stage_pass_claim': False}).encode('utf-8')
  6363	        path = Path(self.core.run_root) / 'temp' / 'native' / 'custody-terminal.json'
  6364	        try:
  6365	            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  6366	            path.write_bytes(raw)
  6367	        except OSError:
  6368	            return False
  6369	        return True
  6370	
  6371	
  6372	# -- the fixed entry of the broker -----------------------------------------
  6373	
  6374	def build_broker_command(*, plan_path, plan_sha256, run_root, nonce, python=None):
  6375	    """The fixed command of the broker.
  6376	
  6377	    The command starts exactly this broker with one sealed plan document.
  6378	    There is no dynamic import of a caller supplied module and no arbitrary
  6379	    argument vector: the only arguments are the plan, its digest, the run
  6380	    root of the round and the nonce of the session.
  6381	    """
  6382	    script = Path(__file__).resolve()
  6383	    if not script.is_file():
  6384	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_SCRIPT_MISSING')
  6385	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', str(plan_sha256)):
  6386	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_INVALID')
  6387	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
  6388	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
  6389	    executable = python or sys.executable
  6390	    return [executable, '-I', '-B', str(script),
  6391	            '--plan', str(plan_path), '--plan-sha256', str(plan_sha256),
  6392	            '--run-root', str(run_root), '--nonce', str(nonce)]
  6393	
  6394	
  6395	class BrokerProcess:
  6396	    """The original handle of the broker process of one round.
  6397	
  6398	    The parent keeps the process handle, the pipes of the private channel
  6399	    and the identity of the executable.  The replies of the broker are
  6400	    read through one bounded background reader, so the parent never blocks
  6401	    on the channel and the deadline of the round is never held up by a
  6402	    silent broker.
  6403	
  6404	    The handle is **deadline aware**: it says so with its own marker and
  6405	    it takes the absolute deadline of every write.  A handle that is not
  6406	    deadline aware is never assumed to be deadline aware by the parent.
  6407	    """
  6408	
  6409	    #: The marker of a handle that understands an absolute deadline.
  6410	    channel_deadline_aware = True
  6411	
  6412	    def __init__(self, process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
  6413	                 clock=None, context=None):
  6414	        self._process = process
  6415	        self._channel = channel
  6416	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  6417	        # The ONE context of the launch.  The close of the broker reads
  6418	        # the absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of
  6419	        # the round from it and it never opens a budget of its own.
  6420	        self._context = context
  6421	        self.pid = process.pid
  6422	        self.executable = executable
  6423	        self.executable_sha256 = executable_sha256
  6424	        # Whether the reader of the channel really stopped when the channel
  6425	        # was closed.  It is ``None`` until the close happened and it stays
  6426	        # ``False`` when the reader was still alive after the bounded join.
  6427	        self.reader_finished = None
  6428	        # The absolute deadline of the round, handed over by the parent.
  6429	        # The parent may only shorten it.
  6430	        self.write_deadline = None
  6431	        self.close_facts = None
  6432	
  6433	    def bind_channel_deadline(self, deadline):
  6434	        """Bind the absolute deadline of the round to the private channel.
  6435	
  6436	        The deadline is a finite number of seconds of the monotonic clock
  6437	        and it may only shorten the deadline that is already bound: a
  6438	        later deadline is refused.
  6439	        """
  6440	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  6441	        if value is None:
  6442	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_INVALID')
  6443	        if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
  6444	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
  6445	        self.write_deadline = value
  6446	        binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
  6447	        if callable(binder):
  6448	            binder(value)
  6449	        return value
  6450	
  6451	    def write(self, raw, deadline=None):
  6452	        """Write one frame of the parent, bounded by the deadline.
  6453	
  6454	        The deadline is explicit and it is checked: a write that is not
  6455	        bounded by an absolute deadline would let a broker that never
  6456	        reads hold the supervisor of the round forever.
  6457	        """
  6458	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  6459	        if value is None:
  6460	            value = self.write_deadline
  6461	        if value is None:
  6462	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_MISSING')
  6463	        if self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
  6464	            raise BrokerError('BROKER_DEADLINE_EXTENDED')
  6465	        return self._channel.write(raw, value)
  6466	
  6467	    def read(self, timeout):
  6468	        return self._channel.read(timeout)
  6469	
  6470	    def close(self, deadline=None):
  6471	        """Close the original handle of the broker.
  6472	
  6473	        The close happens even when the exchange of the stop failed: the
  6474	        resource of this round is released either way.
  6475	
  6476	        The facts of the close are returned and they are named: a handle
  6477	        that was not closed, a reader that did not stop and a process that
  6478	        did not exit are facts of the round and they are never hidden
  6479	        behind a success.  The release of the original process handle is
  6480	        explicit and it is never left to the garbage collector.
  6481	
  6482	        The waits of the close are bounded by **one** absolute deadline:
  6483	        the deadline that the parent bound before it closed the handle.
  6484	        The remaining time is recomputed before every wait, so that a
  6485	        wait that already consumed part of the budget never gets the
  6486	        whole budget again and no layer of the cleanup adds a budget of
  6487	        its own.
  6488	
  6489	        The close may be **repeated** inside the same deadline: an item
  6490	        that was confirmed is never closed a second time and an item
  6491	        that was not confirmed is coordinated again.  The attempt that
  6492	        ended is not a release: only the facts of the close prove it.
  6493	
  6494	        The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
  6495	        round and not a fact of the last attempt: a repeated close keeps
  6496	        the first error that the reader really had, it publishes the
  6497	        source of that error and it never turns a real error into a
  6498	        ``None``.  The resource facts of the close may converge while
  6499	        the error history of the reader stays: a close that returned is
  6500	        not a release and a retry is not an amnesty.
  6501	
  6502	        The **endpoint is the only owner** of the files of the channel.
  6503	        The broker process therefore never closes the standard input and
  6504	        the standard output of the process by itself: it closes the
  6505	        channel, and the channel closes the files that it owns.
  6506	        """
  6507	        value = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  6508	        budget = max(0.0, value - self._clock())
  6509	        facts = {'channel_closed': False,
  6510	                 'channel_reader_finished': None,
  6511	                 'channel_reader_error': None,
  6512	                 'channel_reader_error_source': None,
  6513	                 'channel_reader_secondary_errors': [],
  6514	                 'channel_overflow': False,
  6515	                 'channel_overflow_facts': {},
  6516	                 'close_cancellation': None,
  6517	                 'channel_retained_workers': 0,
  6518	                 'write_end_closed': False,
  6519	                 'read_end_closed': False,
  6520	                 'stdin_closed': False,
  6521	                 'stdout_closed': False,
  6522	                 'process_exited': False,
  6523	                 'exit_code': None,
  6524	                 'terminated': False,
  6525	                 'process_handle_released': False,
  6526	                 'process_handle_release_method': None,
  6527	                 'worker_thread_handle_released': False,
  6528	                 'cleanup_budget_seconds': budget,
  6529	                 'cleanup_deadline': value}
  6530	        # The facts of a previous attempt are kept: an item that was
  6531	        # confirmed is never closed a second time and its fact stays
  6532	        # true.
  6533	        previous = self.close_facts if isinstance(self.close_facts, dict) else {}
  6534	        for key, confirmed in previous.items():
  6535	            if key in ('cleanup_budget_seconds', 'cleanup_deadline'):
  6536	                continue
  6537	            if (confirmed is True
  6538	                    or (key == 'exit_code' and confirmed is not None)
  6539	                    or key == 'process_handle_release_method'
  6540	                    or key == 'close_cancellation'):
  6541	                facts[key] = confirmed
  6542	        # The channel and its endpoints use the very same absolute
  6543	        # deadline: they never start a wait of their own.
  6544	        binder = getattr(self._channel, 'bind_deadline', None)
  6545	        if callable(binder):
  6546	            try:
  6547	                binder(value)
  6548	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6549	                pass
  6550	        if not facts['channel_closed']:
  6551	            channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
  6552	        elif not self._channel_complete(facts):
  6553	            # A previous attempt already called the close of the
  6554	            # channel, but it did not confirm **every** item of it: the
  6555	            # read end, the worker of an endpoint and the original
  6556	            # handle of its thread may still be open.  The items that
  6557	            # were not confirmed are coordinated again, inside the very
  6558	            # same deadline.  A close that returned is not a release.
  6559	            channel_facts = self._close_channel(facts)
  6560	        else:
  6561	            channel_facts = None
  6562	        if channel_facts is not None:
  6563	            facts.update(channel_facts)
  6564	        # The error of the reader of the channel is a **history** of the
  6565	        # round: a repeated close never turns a real error of the reader
  6566	        # into a ``None``.  The first error is the error of the round and
  6567	        # a later error is kept beside it as a secondary one.
  6568	        self._keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous)
  6569	        # The files of the channel belong to the endpoints of the
  6570	        # channel.  The fact of their close is the fact of the channel.
  6571	        facts['stdin_closed'] = facts['write_end_closed']
  6572	        facts['stdout_closed'] = facts['read_end_closed']
  6573	        if not facts['process_exited']:
  6574	            try:
  6575	                # The bound of the cleanup is read **again** here: the
  6576	                # close of the channel may have published a shorter
  6577	                # cleanup deadline of the round and the wait of the
  6578	                # process receives the time that is really left.
  6579	                current = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  6580	                self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, current - self._clock()))
  6581	                facts['process_exited'] = True
  6582	                facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
  6583	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6584	                # Only the broker that this parent holds is terminated, and
  6585	                # its exit is confirmed afterwards with a bounded wait.
  6586	                try:
  6587	                    self._process.terminate()
  6588	                    facts['terminated'] = True
  6589	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6590	                    pass
  6591	                try:
  6592	                    # The bound is read **again** for the retry as well:
  6593	                    # a deadline that was published while the first wait
  6594	                    # ran shortens the retry too.
  6595	                    current = self._cleanup_deadline(deadline)
  6596	                    self._process.wait(timeout=max(0.0, current - self._clock()))
  6597	                    facts['process_exited'] = True
  6598	                    facts['exit_code'] = self._process.returncode
  6599	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6600	                    pass
  6601	        if not facts['process_handle_released']:
  6602	            facts.update(self._release_process_handle(facts['process_exited']))
  6603	        self.close_facts = facts
  6604	        self.reader_finished = facts['channel_reader_finished']
  6605	        return facts
  6606	
  6607	    @staticmethod
  6608	    def _keep_reader_error_history(facts, previous):
  6609	        """Keep the history of the error of the reader of the channel.
  6610	
  6611	        The **first** error that the reader of the channel really had is
  6612	        the error of the round: every later close keeps it and it is
  6613	        never turned into a ``None`` by a repeated close.  The resource
  6614	        facts of the close may converge, but a real error of the reader
  6615	        is never lost by the retry: the copy of the facts of a previous
  6616	        attempt keeps the facts that are ``True`` and it does **not**
  6617	        keep a code, so the error is restored here from the history
  6618	        itself.
  6619	
  6620	        A later error is a **secondary** error: it is kept beside the
  6621	        first one and it never replaces it.  The source of the error is
  6622	        published with it: ``FIRST_CLOSE`` when the close that is running
  6623	        now is the one that really observed the error and
  6624	        ``REPEATED_CLOSE`` when this attempt carries the error of an
  6625	        earlier one.  The fact of the error itself is the history and it
  6626	        is never replaced by a boolean.
  6627	
  6628	        The named fact of the cancellation of the close is independent
  6629	        from this history and it is never merged with it.
  6630	        """
  6631	        previous = previous if isinstance(previous, dict) else {}
  6632	        first = previous.get('channel_reader_error')
  6633	        current = facts.get('channel_reader_error')
  6634	        secondary = list(previous.get('channel_reader_secondary_errors') or [])
  6635	        if first:
  6636	            if current and current != first:
  6637	                # A second error of the reader is kept beside the first
  6638	                # one: the first error stays the error of the round.
  6639	                if current not in secondary:
  6640	                    secondary.append(current)
  6641	            facts['channel_reader_secondary_errors'] = secondary
  6642	            facts['channel_reader_error'] = first
  6643	            facts['channel_reader_error_source'] = 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
  6644	            return facts
  6645	        if current:
  6646	            facts['channel_reader_error_source'] = 'FIRST_CLOSE'
  6647	        return facts
  6648	
  6649	    @staticmethod
  6650	    def _channel_complete(facts):
  6651	        """Whether the close of the channel confirmed every item of it.
  6652	
  6653	        The items are the two ends of the channel, the reader of the
  6654	        channel, the workers of the endpoints and the original handles
  6655	        of their threads.  A fact that is missing is a missing fact and
  6656	        not a success.
  6657	        """
  6658	        if facts.get('channel_reader_finished') is not True:
  6659	            return False
  6660	        if facts.get('read_end_closed') is not True:
  6661	            return False
  6662	        if facts.get('write_end_closed') is not True:
  6663	            return False
  6664	        if int(facts.get('channel_retained_workers') or 0) != 0:
  6665	            return False
  6666	        if facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released') is not True:
  6667	            return False
  6668	        return True
  6669	
  6670	    def _close_channel(self, facts):
  6671	        """Close the channel of the round and report its facts.
  6672	
  6673	        The method returns the facts of the close of the channel and it
  6674	        never raises: the failure of the close of the channel is a fact
  6675	        of the round and it is named in the facts.
  6676	        """
  6677	        try:
  6678	            channel_facts = self._channel.close()
  6679	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6680	            facts['channel_closed'] = False
  6681	            return None
  6682	        facts['channel_closed'] = True
  6683	        facts['channel_reader_finished'] = getattr(self._channel,
  6684	                                                    'reader_finished', None)
  6685	        if not isinstance(channel_facts, dict):
  6686	            return None
  6687	        facts['write_end_closed'] = bool(channel_facts.get('write_end_closed'))
  6688	        facts['read_end_closed'] = bool(channel_facts.get('read_end_closed'))
  6689	        facts['channel_reader_error'] = channel_facts.get('reader_error')
  6690	        # The overflow of the channel is a **named** failure of the
  6691	        # round and it travels with the facts of the close: a channel
  6692	        # that overflowed is never a channel that ended normally.
  6693	        facts['channel_overflow'] = bool(channel_facts.get('overflow'))
  6694	        facts['channel_overflow_facts'] = dict(
  6695	            channel_facts.get('overflow_facts') or {})
  6696	        facts['channel_retained_workers'] = int(
  6697	            (channel_facts.get('write_retained_workers') or 0)
  6698	            + (channel_facts.get('read_retained_workers') or 0))
  6699	        facts['worker_thread_handle_released'] = bool(
  6700	            channel_facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released'))
  6701	        return channel_facts
  6702	
  6703	    def _cleanup_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  6704	        """The ONE absolute deadline of the cleanup of the broker.
  6705	
  6706	        The deadline is the one that the parent bound before it closed
  6707	        the handle.  It may only be shortened, never extended, and every
  6708	        wait of the cleanup recomputes the time that is left.
  6709	
  6710	        The **context** of the launch is read as well: a cleanup that
  6711	        runs after the round published its cleanup deadline uses that
  6712	        deadline and it never falls back to a fresh budget of its own.
  6713	        """
  6714	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  6715	        if value is None:
  6716	            value = self.write_deadline
  6717	        context = self._context
  6718	        if context is not None:
  6719	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
  6720	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
  6721	                if candidate is None:
  6722	                    continue
  6723	                if value is None or candidate < value:
  6724	                    value = candidate
  6725	        if value is None:
  6726	            value = self._clock() + CLEANUP_BUDGET_SECONDS
  6727	        elif self.write_deadline is not None and value > self.write_deadline:
  6728	            value = self.write_deadline
  6729	        return value
  6730	
  6731	    def _release_process_handle(self, exited=False):
  6732	        """Release the original process handle, explicitly and by platform.
  6733	
  6734	        The release is recorded and it is never left to the garbage
  6735	        collector: on Windows the original handle of the process is closed
  6736	        through its own handle object and on POSIX the child process has
  6737	        no separate handle that the parent owns.
  6738	
  6739	        The original handle of the process is closed **only** when the
  6740	        exit of the process was really confirmed.  A process whose exit
  6741	        was not confirmed may still be alive and its original handle is
  6742	        the only handle that the parent owns for it: the handle stays
  6743	        with its owner and a later close may release it.  A close that
  6744	        succeeds is never a proof that the process ended.
  6745	        """
  6746	        if not exited:
  6747	            return {'process_handle_released': False,
  6748	                    'process_handle_release_method': 'EXIT_NOT_CONFIRMED'}
  6749	        if os.name == 'nt':
  6750	            handle = getattr(self._process, '_handle', None)
  6751	            close = getattr(handle, 'Close', None)
  6752	            if close is None:
  6753	                return {'process_handle_released': False,
  6754	                        'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_NO_EXPLICIT_HANDLE'}
  6755	            try:
  6756	                close()
  6757	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  6758	                return {'process_handle_released': False,
  6759	                        'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_HANDLE_CLOSE_FAILED'}
  6760	            return {'process_handle_released': True,
  6761	                    'process_handle_release_method': 'WINDOWS_HANDLE_CLOSE'}
  6762	        return {'process_handle_released': True,
  6763	                'process_handle_release_method': 'POSIX_PROCESS_EXIT'}
  6764	
  6765	
  6766	class _LaunchProgress:
  6767	    """The **monotonic** progress record of the original resources of a launch.
  6768	
  6769	    Every fact of this record is written **immediately** after it was
  6770	    really confirmed and it is never rewritten: the first value of a
  6771	    fact is the only value of it.  The record belongs to the **owner**
  6772	    of the launch and it outlives an interruption of the release: a
  6773	    later recovery of the same owner reads the facts that were already
  6774	    confirmed and it never repeats them.
  6775	
  6776	    The record is the reason why a helper that is interrupted before it
  6777	    returns does not lose the work that it really did.  A return value
  6778	    of a helper is not a fact: the facts are written **before** the
  6779	    next cancellable step and not after the return.
  6780	    """
  6781	
  6782	    def __init__(self):
  6783	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
  6784	        self._facts = {}
  6785	
  6786	    def record(self, key, **facts):
  6787	        """Record the confirmed facts of one original resource."""
  6788	        with self._lock:
  6789	            record = self._facts.setdefault(key, {})
  6790	            for name, value in facts.items():
  6791	                record.setdefault(name, value)
  6792	            return dict(record)
  6793	
  6794	    def fact(self, key):
  6795	        """The confirmed facts that were recorded for one resource."""
  6796	        with self._lock:
  6797	            return dict(self._facts.get(key) or {})
  6798	
  6799	
  6800	class _LaunchAggregate:
  6801	    """The thin persistent owner of **one** launch.
  6802	
  6803	    Every launch of this package has exactly one aggregate and it is
  6804	    created **before** the first side effect of the launch.  It holds
  6805	    the original references of the launch: the process, the channel,
  6806	    the partial endpoints, the workers of the endpoints and the context
  6807	    of the launch.
  6808	
  6809	    The aggregate is the entry that the outer owner of a failed launch
  6810	    uses.  A construction that did not return, a channel whose reader
  6811	    thread did not start and a channel that could not be handed over
  6812	    are found **here** and never through a local variable that is
  6813	    ``None`` at that moment.
  6814	
  6815	    The release of the aggregate is ordered by the **dependency**: the
  6816	    channel owns the files of the process and the worker of an endpoint
  6817	    owns the handle of its thread, so the channel is coordinated first
  6818	    and the process is coordinated after it.  A process whose channel
  6819	    took the files is never closed by the process helper: a file that
  6820	    is closed under a live worker is a file that a worker reads from.
  6821	    """
  6822	
  6823	    def __init__(self, *, launch_id=None, custody=None, clock=None,
  6824	                 context=None):
  6825	        self.launch_id = launch_id or 'launch'
  6826	        self.custody = custody
  6827	        self._clock = clock or time.monotonic
  6828	        if context is not None and clock is None:
  6829	            # The launch shares the clock of the **owner** of its
  6830	            # context: a deadline of the round is measured with the
  6831	            # clock of that round and never with the clock of another
  6832	            # domain.
  6833	            self._clock = getattr(context, 'clock', None) or self._clock
  6834	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
  6835	        self.process = None
  6836	        self.channel = None
  6837	        self.endpoints = []
  6838	        self.workers = []
  6839	        # The **original raw streams** of the launch that could not be
  6840	        # closed.  A raw stream is a file of the process of the round:
  6841	        # the launch that created it is its owner and it is retried by
  6842	        # that owner alone.  A stream that is not in this list was
  6843	        # closed with a confirmed close.
  6844	        self.raw_streams = []
  6845	        self.context = context
  6846	        self.facts = {}
  6847	        # The **progress record** of the original resources of this
  6848	        # launch.  Every confirmed fact - the exit of the process, the
  6849	        # release of its original handle and the confirmed close of
  6850	        # each of its original raw streams - is written into it
  6851	        # **immediately** after its confirmation.  The record is the
  6852	        # single owner of those facts: the helper of the process and the
  6853	        # retry of the raw streams read it and they never close the same
  6854	        # original twice.
  6855	        self.progress = _LaunchProgress()
  6856	        # Whether the exit of the process of this launch was already
  6857	        # **confirmed**.  A retry of the same owner coordinates only the
  6858	        # parts that were not confirmed yet: it never terminates and
  6859	        # never waits a second time.
  6860	        self.process_exit_confirmed = False
  6861	
  6862	    # -- the bindings of the launch ---------------------------------------
  6863	    def bind_process(self, process):
  6864	        """Bind the original process of this launch."""
  6865	        with self._lock:
  6866	            self.process = process
  6867	        return process
  6868	
  6869	    def bind_channel(self, channel):
  6870	        """Bind the channel of this launch."""
  6871	        with self._lock:
  6872	            if channel is not None:
  6873	                self.channel = channel
  6874	        return channel
  6875	
  6876	    def bind_endpoint(self, endpoint):
  6877	        """Bind one endpoint of this launch."""
  6878	        with self._lock:
  6879	            self.endpoints.append(endpoint)
  6880	        return endpoint
  6881	
  6882	    def bind_worker(self, worker):
  6883	        """Bind one worker of this launch."""
  6884	        with self._lock:
  6885	            self.workers.append(worker)
  6886	        return worker
  6887	
  6888	    def bind_context(self, context):
  6889	        """Bind the ONE context of this launch."""
  6890	        with self._lock:
  6891	            if context is not None:
  6892	                self.context = context
  6893	        return self.context
  6894	
  6895	    # -- the custody protocol of the launcher -----------------------------
  6896	    def retain(self, kind, resource, detail=None):
  6897	        """Take one original resource of this launch.
  6898	
  6899	        The protocol is the protocol of the table of the launcher: the
  6900	        channel of a launch registers itself **before** it builds
  6901	        anything, so that a partial channel is always reachable.
  6902	        """
  6903	        with self._lock:
  6904	            if kind == 'channel' and resource is not None:
  6905	                self.channel = resource
  6906	            elif kind == 'endpoint':
  6907	                self.endpoints.append(resource)
  6908	            elif kind == 'worker':
  6909	                self.workers.append(resource)
  6910	        return resource
  6911	
  6912	    def forget(self, resource):
  6913	        """Leave the launch: the resource was handed over or released."""
  6914	        with self._lock:
  6915	            if self.channel is resource:
  6916	                self.channel = None
  6917	            self.endpoints = [item for item in self.endpoints
  6918	                              if item is not resource]
  6919	            self.workers = [item for item in self.workers
  6920	                            if item is not resource]
  6921	        return None
  6922	
  6923	    def items(self):
  6924	        """The original resources that this launch still owns."""
  6925	        with self._lock:
  6926	            return {'process': self.process, 'channel': self.channel,
  6927	                    'endpoints': list(self.endpoints),
  6928	                    'workers': list(self.workers),
  6929	                    'raw_streams': list(self.raw_streams)}
  6930	
  6931	    # -- the ownership of the original raw streams -------------------------
  6932	    def retain_raw_stream(self, name, stream):
  6933	        """Take the ownership of one original raw stream of this launch.
  6934	
  6935	        A raw stream is a file of the process of the round.  It enters
  6936	        the ownership of **this** launch when its close could not be
  6937	        confirmed: the launch is then the only owner that may retry it
  6938	        and the table of the launcher never releases it behind the back
  6939	        of the launch.
  6940	        """
  6941	        with self._lock:
  6942	            for item in self.raw_streams:
  6943	                if item['stream'] is stream:
  6944	                    return item
  6945	            item = {'name': name, 'stream': stream}
  6946	            self.raw_streams.append(item)
  6947	            return item
  6948	
  6949	    def forget_raw_stream(self, stream):
  6950	        """Leave the ownership of one raw stream: its close was confirmed."""
  6951	        with self._lock:
  6952	            self.raw_streams = [item for item in self.raw_streams
  6953	                                if item['stream'] is not stream]
  6954	        return None
  6955	
  6956	    def pending_raw_streams(self):
  6957	        """The original raw streams that this launch still owns."""
  6958	        with self._lock:
  6959	            return list(self.raw_streams)
  6960	
  6961	    # -- the coordinated release ------------------------------------------
  6962	    def _release_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  6963	        """The absolute deadline that the release of this launch uses.
  6964	
  6965	        The deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the **original**
  6966	        context of this launch are one value: the earliest of them.  The
  6967	        context is read **again** at the moment of the release and it is
  6968	        never copied into a number of the launch: a layer that copied
  6969	        the number of a deadline would have a deadline of its own.
  6970	        """
  6971	        return self._release_bound(deadline)[0]
  6972	
  6973	    def _release_bound(self, deadline=None, clock=None):
  6974	        """The absolute deadline of this release and the clock of it.
  6975	
  6976	        The deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the **original**
  6977	        context of this launch are one value: the earliest of them.  The
  6978	        clock of the domain of that value is returned with it: a
  6979	        deadline that belongs to the context of the round is compared in
  6980	        the clock of that context and never in the clock of another
  6981	        domain.  A facade that shares the deadline of the owner and not
  6982	        its clock compares two numbers of two domains.
  6983	        """
  6984	        try:
  6985	            value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  6986	        except BrokerError:
  6987	            value = None
  6988	        owner_clock = clock or self._clock
  6989	        context = self.context
  6990	        if context is not None:
  6991	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
  6992	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
  6993	                if candidate is None:
  6994	                    continue
  6995	                if value is None or candidate < value:
  6996	                    value = candidate
  6997	                    owner_clock = getattr(context, 'clock', None) or owner_clock
  6998	        return value, owner_clock
  6999	
  7000	    def release(self, deadline=None, clock=None):
  7001	        """Coordinate the resources of this launch, in the dependency order.
  7002	
  7003	        The channel is coordinated **first** and the process is
  7004	        coordinated after it.  A close that returned is not a release:
  7005	        the channel is released only when **every** fact of it is
  7006	        closed - the reader, the two ends, the workers and the original
  7007	        handles of their threads.  An incomplete close keeps the
  7008	        channel with its owner and it is named.
  7009	
  7010	        The original handle of the process is released only after the
  7011	        exit of the process was really confirmed.
  7012	
  7013	        The release never raises before every safe step was executed:
  7014	        the original cancellation of the launch is raised at the end and
  7015	        the ordinary failures are named beside it.
  7016	        """
  7017	        released = []
  7018	        retained = []
  7019	        failures = {}
  7020	        cancellations = []
  7021	        # The original raw streams that this launch already owns and
  7022	        # whose close was not confirmed yet: they are retried by **this**
  7023	        # owner and by no other one.
  7024	        pending_raw = self.pending_raw_streams()
  7025	        channel = self.channel
  7026	        if channel is not None:
  7027	            facts = {}
  7028	            try:
  7029	                facts = channel.close()
  7030	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  7031	                cancellations.append(error)
  7032	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7033	                failures['channel'] = type(error).__name__
  7034	            if not isinstance(facts, dict):
  7035	                facts = {}
  7036	            complete = (bool(facts.get('reader_finished'))
  7037	                        and bool(facts.get('read_end_closed'))
  7038	                        and bool(facts.get('write_end_closed'))
  7039	                        and int(facts.get('channel_retained_workers') or 0) == 0
  7040	                        and bool(facts.get('worker_thread_handle_released')))
  7041	            if complete:
  7042	                released.append('channel')
  7043	                self.forget(channel)
  7044	                if self.custody is not None:
  7045	                    self.custody.forget(channel)
  7046	            else:
  7047	                # A close that returned is not a release: the channel
  7048	                # keeps its owner and its original reference.
  7049	                failures['channel'] = 'BROKER_CHANNEL_CLOSE_INCOMPLETE'
  7050	                retained.append('channel')
  7051	                if self.custody is not None:
  7052	                    self.custody.retain('channel', channel,
  7053	                                         'release_incomplete', launch=self)
  7054	        process = self.process
  7055	        if process is not None:
  7056	            # The files of the process belong to the channel when the
  7057	            # channel took them: the process helper never closes them.
  7058	            close_pipes = channel is None
  7059	            outcome = {'failures': {}, 'exited': False, 'retained_streams': []}
  7060	            # The original raw streams that **this** launch owns are
  7061	            # closed by the retry of the raw streams below and by no
  7062	            # other route: the helper of the process and the retry
  7063	            # never close the same original.
  7064	            owned = {item['name']: item['stream'] for item in pending_raw}
  7065	            # The deadline of the launch is read **again** after the
  7066	            # channel was coordinated: a cleanup deadline that was
  7067	            # published in the meantime shortens the wait of the
  7068	            # process and it is never a number that was copied once.
  7069	            bound, bound_clock = self._release_bound(deadline, clock)
  7070	            # The original references that the helper could not confirm
  7071	            # are collected **outside** of its report: a helper that is
  7072	            # interrupted before it returns does not return its report
  7073	            # and the unconfirmed references may not be lost with it.
  7074	            helper_retained = []
  7075	            try:
  7076	                outcome = _release_launch_process(
  7077	                    process, deadline=bound,
  7078	                    close_pipes=close_pipes,
  7079	                    exited=self.process_exit_confirmed,
  7080	                    context=self.context, progress=self.progress,
  7081	                    owned_streams=owned, clock=bound_clock,
  7082	                    retained_out=helper_retained)
  7083	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  7084	                cancellations.append(error)
  7085	            except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7086	                outcome['failures']['process'] = type(error).__name__
  7087	            failures.update(outcome['failures'])
  7088	            # The **facts** of the process are read from the progress
  7089	            # record of this launch and not only from the return value
  7090	            # of the helper: a helper that was interrupted before it
  7091	            # returned does not lose the exit or the handle that it
  7092	            # really confirmed.
  7093	            record = self.progress.fact('process')
  7094	            if record.get('exit_confirmed'):
  7095	                self.process_exit_confirmed = True
  7096	            # A raw stream that could not be closed stays a reachable
  7097	            # owned resource: it keeps its **original** reference and it
  7098	            # is never replaced by a summary of the failure.  This
  7099	            # launch is the owner of it and it retries it.
  7100	            retained_raw = list(outcome.get('retained_streams') or [])
  7101	            retained_raw.extend(helper_retained)
  7102	            for name, stream in retained_raw:
  7103	                retained.append(name)
  7104	                self.retain_raw_stream(name, stream)
  7105	                if self.custody is not None:
  7106	                    self.custody.retain(name, stream, 'close_failed',
  7107	                                        launch=self)
  7108	            # The original process is forgotten **only** when its exit
  7109	            # was confirmed and the original handle of the process was
  7110	            # released.  A close of the handle that failed keeps the
  7111	            # process with its owner and the same owner may retry it.
  7112	            handle_released = bool(record.get('handle_released'))
  7113	            if self.process_exit_confirmed and handle_released:
  7114	                released.append('process')
  7115	                with self._lock:
  7116	                    self.process = None
  7117	                if self.custody is not None:
  7118	                    self.custody.forget(process)
  7119	            else:
  7120	                retained.append('process')
  7121	                if self.custody is not None:
  7122	                    self.custody.retain('process', process, 'release_failed',
  7123	                                         launch=self)
  7124	        # The retry of the original raw streams that this launch owns.
  7125	        # The retry happens **after** the channel and the process were
  7126	        # coordinated: a raw file of a process whose channel took the
  7127	        # files is never closed by the launch.
  7128	        self._release_raw_streams(pending_raw, released, retained, failures,
  7129	                                  cancellations, deadline, clock)
  7130	        self.facts = {'released': list(released), 'retained': list(retained),
  7131	                      'failures': dict(failures)}
  7132	        if cancellations:
  7133	            # The original cancellation is raised after every safe step
  7134	            # of the release was executed and the ordinary failures stay
  7135	            # named next to it.
  7136	            raise cancellations[0]
  7137	        return self.facts
  7138	
  7139	    def _release_raw_streams(self, items, released, retained, failures,
  7140	                             cancellations, deadline, clock=None):
  7141	        """Retry the original raw streams that this launch still owns.
  7142	
  7143	        A raw stream is a file of the process of the round.  It is owned
  7144	        by the launch that created it and it is retried **only** by that
  7145	        owner: the table of the launcher never closes it and it never
  7146	        becomes a detached entry that nobody may reach.
  7147	
  7148	        The retry happens under the **current** bound of the release:
  7149	        the deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the original
  7150	        context of the launch are read again **before every close** and
  7151	        an expired bound never starts a new close.
  7152	
  7153	        A stream that is owned by a channel - a live channel or a
  7154	        partial one - is never closed by the launch: the files of the
  7155	        process belong to the channel and a file that is closed under a
  7156	        live worker is a file that a worker reads from.
  7157	
  7158	        A stream whose close is confirmed is forgotten **before** it
  7159	        leaves the table of the launcher: the confirmed close is
  7160	        recorded in the progress record of this launch and the table
  7161	        never resurrects the stream.  A confirmed close is never
  7162	        repeated by a later release of the same owner.
  7163	        """
  7164	        if not items:
  7165	            return
  7166	        if self.channel is not None:
  7167	            # The channel owns the files of the process: the raw
  7168	            # streams are never closed under a live or a partial
  7169	            # channel endpoint.
  7170	            for item in items:
  7171	                retained.append(item['name'])
  7172	                failures['raw_stream'] = 'BROKER_RAW_STREAM_OWNED_BY_CHANNEL'
  7173	            return
  7174	        for item in items:
  7175	            stream = item['stream']
  7176	            name = item['name']
  7177	            # The confirmed close of an original is **never** repeated:
  7178	            # the progress record of this launch is the owner of that
  7179	            # fact and it outlives every later release.
  7180	            record = self.progress.fact('stream:' + name)
  7181	            if record.get('stream') is stream and record.get('closed'):
  7182	                self.forget_raw_stream(stream)
  7183	                if self.custody is not None:
  7184	                    self.custody.forget(stream)
  7185	                released.append(name)
  7186	                continue
  7187	            # The bound of the release is read **again** before **each**
  7188	            # close: a bound that was tightened while the previous close
  7189	            # ran refuses this one and it never opens a cleanup lease of
  7190	            # its own.
  7191	            value, bound_clock = self._release_bound(deadline, clock)
  7192	            if value is not None and bound_clock() >= value:
  7193	                # An expired bound never starts a new close: the stream
  7194	                # keeps its owner and the round keeps the fact.
  7195	                failures['raw_stream'] = 'BROKER_RAW_STREAM_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'
  7196	                retained.append(name)
  7197	                continue
  7198	            try:
  7199	                stream.close()
  7200	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  7201	                # The original cancellation survives: it is raised after
  7202	                # every safe step of the release and the stream keeps
  7203	                # its owner.
  7204	                cancellations.append(error)
  7205	                failures['raw_stream'] = 'CANCELLED'
  7206	                retained.append(name)
  7207	                continue
  7208	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7209	                failures['raw_stream'] = type(error).__name__
  7210	                retained.append(name)
  7211	                continue
  7212	            # The close was confirmed: the fact is recorded **before**
  7213	            # the next cancellable step and the stream is forgotten by
  7214	            # its owner and by the table of the launcher.
  7215	            self.progress.record('stream:' + name, stream=stream, closed=True)
  7216	            self.forget_raw_stream(stream)
  7217	            if self.custody is not None:
  7218	                self.custody.forget(stream)
  7219	            released.append(name)
  7220	
  7221	
  7222	class _LauncherCustody:
  7223	    """The persistent owner of the original resources of a failed launch.
  7224	
  7225	    The launcher owns the original process of the round until the
  7226	    channel of the round exists.  A channel that could not be built
  7227	    would leave the launcher with a running process and with pipes that
  7228	    nobody owns.
  7229	
  7230	    Every original resource of such a launch enters this table **with
  7231	    its original reference** and it stays there until a confirmed
  7232	    release: the round never loses the ownership of a resource that it
  7233	    created, and a pid is never used to find a process again.
  7234	
  7235	    The table is the recovery entry point of the launcher.  It is
  7236	    explicit and it is finite: a caller that wants the resources of a
  7237	    failed launch released asks for it.
  7238	
  7239	    The resources of a launch are coordinated by the **aggregate** of
  7240	    that launch and not by the order of this table: the channel of a
  7241	    launch is closed before the process of that launch, because the
  7242	    channel owns the files of the process.  A resource that belongs to
  7243	    a launch is never released a second time by this table.
  7244	    """
  7245	
  7246	    def __init__(self):
  7247	        self._items = []
  7248	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
  7249	        # The ONE context of the round, when the table is bound to it.
  7250	        # A recovery that runs without a deadline of its own reads the
  7251	        # absolute deadline and the published cleanup deadline of the
  7252	        # round from it and it never opens a fresh budget.
  7253	        self._context = None
  7254	
  7255	    def bind_context(self, context):
  7256	        """Bind the ONE context of the round to this table."""
  7257	        if context is not None:
  7258	            self._context = context
  7259	        return self._context
  7260	
  7261	    def _effective_deadline(self, deadline=None):
  7262	        """The ONE deadline that a release of this table really uses."""
  7263	        return self._effective_bound(deadline)[0]
  7264	
  7265	    def _effective_bound(self, deadline=None):
  7266	        """The ONE deadline of a release of this table and its clock.
  7267	
  7268	        The clock of the domain of the deadline is returned with it: a
  7269	        deadline that belongs to the context of the round is measured
  7270	        with the clock of that context and never with the clock of
  7271	        another domain.
  7272	        """
  7273	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  7274	        owner_clock = time.monotonic
  7275	        context = self._context
  7276	        if context is not None:
  7277	            for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
  7278	                              context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
  7279	                if candidate is None:
  7280	                    continue
  7281	                if value is None or candidate < value:
  7282	                    value = candidate
  7283	                    owner_clock = getattr(context, 'clock', None) or owner_clock
  7284	        return value, owner_clock
  7285	
  7286	    def retain(self, kind, resource, detail=None, launch=None):
  7287	        """Take one original resource into the table of the launcher.
  7288	
  7289	        The table never names the same resource twice: a resource that
  7290	        is already with its owner is not entered a second time and the
  7291	        table converges.
  7292	
  7293	        The ``launch`` is the aggregate that owns the resource.  A
  7294	        resource that belongs to a launch is coordinated by that
  7295	        launch and it is never released a second time by this table.
  7296	        """
  7297	        with self._lock:
  7298	            for item in self._items:
  7299	                if item['kind'] == kind and item['resource'] is resource:
  7300	                    return resource
  7301	            self._items.append({'kind': kind, 'resource': resource,
  7302	                                'detail': detail, 'launch': launch})
  7303	        return resource
  7304	
  7305	    def forget(self, resource):
  7306	        """Leave the table: the resource was handed over to its owner."""
  7307	        with self._lock:
  7308	            remaining = [item for item in self._items
  7309	                         if item['resource'] is not resource]
  7310	            self._items = remaining
  7311	
  7312	    def items(self):
  7313	        """The original resources that the launcher still owns."""
  7314	        with self._lock:
  7315	            return list(self._items)
  7316	
  7317	    def release(self, deadline=None, context=None):
  7318	        """Try to release every original resource of the table.
  7319	
  7320	        The release of every resource is independent of the others: a
  7321	        resource that could not be released stays in the table with its
  7322	        original reference and it is named in the report.
  7323	
  7324	        The **launches** are coordinated first: every aggregate closes
  7325	        the channel of its launch before the process of that launch.
  7326	        The resources that belong to no launch are released afterwards
  7327	        and a resource of a launch is never released twice.
  7328	
  7329	        The wait of every resource uses the **same** absolute deadline.
  7330	        A recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a
  7331	        fixed number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
  7332	        """
  7333	        released = []
  7334	        retained = []
  7335	        cancellations = []
  7336	        with self._lock:
  7337	            items = list(self._items)
  7338	        keep = []
  7339	        for item in items:
  7340	            if item['kind'] == 'aggregate':
  7341	                launch = item['resource']
  7342	                bound, bound_clock = self._effective_bound(deadline)
  7343	                try:
  7344	                    # The deadline of the table is read **again** for
  7345	                    # every launch: a cleanup deadline that was
  7346	                    # published in the meantime shortens the release of
  7347	                    # the launch.  The clock of its domain travels with
  7348	                    # it: a launch never compares a deadline of the
  7349	                    # round with a clock of another domain.
  7350	                    report = launch.release(deadline=bound, clock=bound_clock)
  7351	                except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as error:
  7352	                    cancellations.append(error)
  7353	                    report = {'released': [], 'retained': ['launch']}
  7354	                except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7355	                    report = {'released': [], 'retained': ['launch']}
  7356	                released.extend(report.get('released') or [])
  7357	                names = list(report.get('retained') or [])
  7358	                retained.extend(names)
  7359	                if names:
  7360	                    keep.append(item)
  7361	                continue
  7362	            if item.get('launch') is not None:
  7363	                # The launch of this resource coordinates it: the table
  7364	                # never releases it a second time.  A resource that the
  7365	                # launch released **while** this release was running is
  7366	                # not named by the table any more: the stale snapshot of
  7367	                # the table never resurrects it and never names it as
  7368	                # retained.
  7369	                with self._lock:
  7370	                    owned = any(current['resource'] is item['resource']
  7371	                                for current in self._items)
  7372	                if not owned:
  7373	                    continue
  7374	                keep.append(item)
  7375	                retained.append(item['kind'])
  7376	                continue
  7377	            resource = item['resource']
  7378	            try:
  7379	                if item['kind'] == 'process':
  7380	                    bound, bound_clock = self._effective_bound(deadline)
  7381	                    outcome = _release_launch_process(
  7382	                        resource, deadline=bound, close_pipes=True,
  7383	                        context=self._context, clock=bound_clock)
  7384	                    if outcome['failures']:
  7385	                        keep.append(item)
  7386	                        retained.append(item['kind'])
  7387	                        # A raw stream that could not be closed stays a
  7388	                        # reachable owned resource of the launcher.
  7389	                        for name, stream in outcome.get('retained_streams') or []:
  7390	                            self.retain(name, stream, 'close_failed')
  7391	                        continue
  7392	                else:
  7393	                    close = getattr(resource, 'close', None)
  7394	                    if callable(close):
  7395	                        close()
  7396	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7397	                keep.append(item)
  7398	                retained.append(item['kind'])
  7399	                continue
  7400	            released.append(item['kind'])
  7401	        with self._lock:
  7402	            # The table is rebuilt from the entries that are **still**
  7403	            # in it: an entry that was forgotten while the release ran -
  7404	            # a process that the aggregate of its launch released - is
  7405	            # never resurrected as a detached entry.  The entries that
  7406	            # were added while the release ran are new resources of the
  7407	            # round and they are kept.
  7408	            kept = {id(item['resource']) for item in keep}
  7409	            snapshot = {id(item['resource']) for item in items}
  7410	            self._items = [item for item in self._items
  7411	                           if id(item['resource']) not in snapshot
  7412	                           or id(item['resource']) in kept]
  7413	        if cancellations:
  7414	            raise cancellations[0]
  7415	        return {'released': list(released), 'retained': list(retained)}
  7416	
  7417	
  7418	#: The one table of the original resources of the launches of this
  7419	#: package.  It is empty after a launch that was handed over and it
  7420	#: names the resources of a launch that was not.
  7421	_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY = _LauncherCustody()
  7422	
  7423	
  7424	def release_retained_launches(deadline=None, *, context=None):
  7425	    """The recovery entry point of the launcher of this package.
  7426	
  7427	    The caller asks for the original resources of the launches that were
  7428	    not handed over to be released, and the report names the resources
  7429	    that could not be released.
  7430	
  7431	    The wait of every resource uses the **same** absolute deadline of the
  7432	    round: a recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never
  7433	    waits a fixed number of seconds for a process that may still be
  7434	    alive.
  7435	
  7436	    The deadline of the context is **read again** here: a recovery
  7437	    that runs after the round published its cleanup deadline uses that
  7438	    deadline and it never falls back to a fresh budget of its own.
  7439	    """
  7440	    value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  7441	    if context is not None:
  7442	        _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.bind_context(context)
  7443	    return _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.release(deadline=value)
  7444	
  7445	
  7446	def _launcher_custody_items():
  7447	    """The original resources that the launcher of this package owns.
  7448	
  7449	    The list is a copy and every entry carries the **original** object:
  7450	    a pid is never used to find a process of the round again.
  7451	    """
  7452	    return _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.items()
  7453	
  7454	
  7455	def _release_budget(deadline=None, context=None, clock=None):
  7456	    """The seconds that are left for the wait of a process of a launch.
  7457	
  7458	    The deadline of the caller and the deadlines of the **original**
  7459	    context of the launch are one value: the earliest of them.  The
  7460	    context is read **again** at the moment of the wait: a cleanup
  7461	    deadline that was published while the channel of the launch was
  7462	    closed shortens the wait of the process.  A recovery never opens a
  7463	    budget of its own and it never waits a fixed number of seconds for
  7464	    a process that may still be alive.
  7465
