  7466	    The bound of the package is used only when neither the caller nor
  7467	    the context of the launch brought a deadline at all.
  7468	    """
  7469	    try:
  7470	        value = _require_deadline(deadline)
  7471	    except BrokerError:
  7472	        # A deadline that cannot be understood is no deadline: the
  7473	        # bound of the package is used and it is never a budget that
  7474	        # the caller did not ask for.
  7475	        value = None
  7476	    if context is not None:
  7477	        for candidate in (context.absolute_deadline,
  7478	                          context.cleanup_deadline_value()):
  7479	            if candidate is None:
  7480	                continue
  7481	            if value is None or candidate < value:
  7482	                value = candidate
  7483	    if value is None:
  7484	        return CLOSE_JOIN_SECONDS
  7485	    # The clock is the clock of the domain of the deadline: a deadline
  7486	    # that belongs to the context of the round is measured with the
  7487	    # clock of that context.
  7488	    return max(0.0, value - (clock or time.monotonic)())
  7489	
  7490	
  7491	def _release_launch_process(process, *, deadline=None, close_pipes=True,
  7492	                            exited=None, context=None, progress=None,
  7493	                            owned_streams=None, clock=None, retained_out=None):
  7494	    """Terminate one process of a launch and report the two facts.
  7495	
  7496	    The release is explicit and it is bounded: the process is
  7497	    terminated, its exit is confirmed and its pipes are closed when
  7498	    nobody else owns them.
  7499	
  7500	    The wait uses the **same** absolute deadline of the round: the
  7501	    deadline is read **again** from the context of the launch at the
  7502	    moment of the wait and a recovery never opens a budget of its own.
  7503	
  7504	    The ``exited`` argument is the exit that an **earlier** attempt of
  7505	    the same owner already confirmed: a retry coordinates only the
  7506	    parts that were not confirmed yet and it never terminates and
  7507	    never waits a second time.
  7508	
  7509	    The original handle of the process is closed **only** when its exit
  7510	    was really confirmed.  A process that may still be alive keeps its
  7511	    handle and its owner: the handle is the only thing that may stop
  7512	    it and it is never closed under it.
  7513	
  7514	    Every **confirmed** fact is written into the ``progress`` record of
  7515	    the owner **immediately** after its confirmation and before the next
  7516	    cancellable step.  The record is the reason why an interruption
  7517	    that prevents this helper from returning does not lose the work
  7518	    that it really did: a return value is not a fact.
  7519	
  7520	    The original raw streams that the **owner** already owns are never
  7521	    closed here: the ``owned_streams`` argument names them and the
  7522	    retry of the owner closes them.  The helper and the retry never
  7523	    close the same original and a confirmed close is never repeated.
  7524	
  7525	    The report names every step that did not succeed and it names the
  7526	    **exit** of the process as a separate fact: a close that succeeded
  7527	    is never a proof that the process ended.  A raw stream that could
  7528	    not be closed is returned with its **original** reference: it stays
  7529	    a reachable owned resource and it is never replaced by a summary.
  7530	    """
  7531	    failures = {}
  7532	    retained_streams = []
  7533	    owned = owned_streams if isinstance(owned_streams, dict) else {}
  7534	    if progress is not None:
  7535	        confirmed = bool(progress.fact('process').get('exit_confirmed'))
  7536	    else:
  7537	        confirmed = False
  7538	    confirmed = confirmed or bool(exited)
  7539	    if not confirmed:
  7540	        try:
  7541	            process.terminate()
  7542	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7543	            failures['terminate'] = type(error).__name__
  7544	        budget = _release_budget(deadline, context, clock=clock)
  7545	        try:
  7546	            process.wait(timeout=budget)
  7547	            confirmed = True
  7548	        except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7549	            failures['wait'] = type(error).__name__
  7550	    if confirmed:
  7551	        # The exit is a fact of the owner and it is recorded before the
  7552	        # next cancellable step: a later release of the same owner
  7553	        # never terminates and never waits a second time.
  7554	        if progress is not None:
  7555	            progress.record('process', exit_confirmed=True)
  7556	    if confirmed and os.name == 'nt':
  7557	        # The original handle of the process is closed explicitly and it
  7558	        # is never left to the garbage collector.  A handle that could
  7559	        # not be closed keeps its owner.
  7560	        handle = getattr(process, '_handle', None)
  7561	        closer = getattr(handle, 'Close', None)
  7562	        if callable(closer):
  7563	            try:
  7564	                closer()
  7565	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7566	                failures['handle'] = type(error).__name__
  7567	            else:
  7568	                if progress is not None:
  7569	                    progress.record('process', handle_released=True)
  7570	        elif progress is not None:
  7571	            # There is no original handle to release: the handle of the
  7572	            # process is not a resource of this platform and its
  7573	            # absence is not a failure.
  7574	            progress.record('process', handle_released=True)
  7575	    elif confirmed and progress is not None:
  7576	        progress.record('process', handle_released=True)
  7577	    if close_pipes:
  7578	        for name in ('stdin', 'stdout', 'stderr'):
  7579	            stream = getattr(process, name, None)
  7580	            if stream is None:
  7581	                continue
  7582	            if owned.get(name) is stream:
  7583	                # The owner of this original closed it in its own
  7584	                # retry: the helper never closes it a second time.
  7585	                continue
  7586	            if progress is not None:
  7587	                record = progress.fact('stream:' + name)
  7588	                if record.get('stream') is stream and record.get('closed'):
  7589	                    # The close was confirmed by an earlier release of
  7590	                    # the same owner: it is never repeated.
  7591	                    continue
  7592	            # The bound of the release is read **again** before **each**
  7593	            # close: an expired bound never starts a new close and the
  7594	            # stream keeps its owner.
  7595	            if deadline is not None and (clock or time.monotonic)() >= deadline:
  7596	                failures[name] = 'BROKER_RAW_STREAM_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED'
  7597	                retained_streams.append((name, stream))
  7598	                continue
  7599	            try:
  7600	                stream.close()
  7601	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
  7602	                # The original cancellation survives and the facts that
  7603	                # were confirmed before it are kept: the stream keeps
  7604	                # its owner and its original reference.  The reference
  7605	                # is handed to the owner **before** the cancellation
  7606	                # leaves, because a helper that does not return does
  7607	                # not return its report either.
  7608	                if retained_out is not None:
  7609	                    retained_out.append((name, stream))
  7610	                raise
  7611	            except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7612	                failures[name] = type(error).__name__
  7613	                # The stream keeps its **original** reference: it stays
  7614	                # a reachable owned resource of the round.
  7615	                retained_streams.append((name, stream))
  7616	                continue
  7617	            if progress is not None:
  7618	                progress.record('stream:' + name, stream=stream, closed=True)
  7619	    return {'failures': failures, 'exited': confirmed,
  7620	            'retained_streams': retained_streams}
  7621	
  7622	
  7623	def _terminate_and_close_process(process, *, deadline=None, close_pipes=True,
  7624	                                 context=None):
  7625	    """Terminate one process of this round and close its pipes.
  7626	
  7627	    The release is explicit and it is bounded: the process is
  7628	    terminated, its exit is confirmed and its pipes are closed.  The
  7629	    method never raises: it is the last step of a failure and it never
  7630	    replaces the failure that is on the way out.
  7631	
  7632	    The wait uses the **same** absolute deadline of the round: a
  7633	    recovery never opens a budget of its own and it never waits a fixed
  7634	    number of seconds for a process that may still be alive.
  7635	
  7636	    The original handle of the process is closed **only** when its exit
  7637	    was really confirmed.  A process that may still be alive keeps its
  7638	    handle and its owner: the handle is the only thing that may stop
  7639	    it and it is never closed under it.
  7640	
  7641	    The report names every step that did not succeed, so that the
  7642	    caller can keep the original reference of a resource that is still
  7643	    open.
  7644	    """
  7645	    return _release_launch_process(process, deadline=deadline,
  7646	                                   close_pipes=close_pipes,
  7647	                                   context=context)['failures']
  7648	
  7649	
  7650	def _is_startup_refusal(error):
  7651	    """Whether the error is a refusal of the admission of the launch.
  7652	
  7653	    A refusal of the admission is **not** a failure of the resources of
  7654	    the launch: the process that was already admitted keeps its owner
  7655	    in the table of the launcher and it stays reachable and recoverable
  7656	    by the recovery of the launcher.  The launch never pretends to undo
  7657	    the operating system work that it really admitted and it never
  7658	    skips the registration of its original resource.
  7659	    """
  7660	    code = getattr(error, 'code', None)
  7661	    return code in ('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED',
  7662	                    'BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED')
  7663	
  7664	
  7665	def _adopt_broker_process(process, *, channel_factory, executable,
  7666	                          executable_sha256, deadline=None, aggregate=None,
  7667	                          custody=None):
  7668	    """Take the original process of the broker into the custody of a channel.
  7669	
  7670	    The launcher owns the original process of the round until the
  7671	    channel of the round exists.  A channel that could not be built
  7672	    would leave the launcher with a running process and with pipes that
  7673	    nobody owns.
  7674	
  7675	    The **channel** is the only owner of the files of the process.  A
  7676	    channel that exists and that could not be handed over is closed by
  7677	    the launcher through the channel and never by closing the standard
  7678	    input and the standard output of the process by itself: a file that
  7679	    is closed under a live worker of the channel is a file that a
  7680	    worker is reading from.
  7681	
  7682	    The **aggregate** of the launch is the owner that the outer code
  7683	    uses: a partial channel - a channel whose reader thread did not
  7684	    start - is found through the aggregate and never through a local
  7685	    variable that is ``None`` at that moment.
  7686	
  7687	    A resource that could not be released keeps its **original**
  7688	    reference in the table of the launcher: the session never received
  7689	    the process and the round never loses the ownership of it.
  7690	
  7691	    The handover happens exactly once: the process that the channel
  7692	    received is the process that the parent owns.
  7693	    """
  7694	    table = custody if custody is not None else _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY
  7695	    launch = aggregate if aggregate is not None else _LaunchAggregate(
  7696	        custody=table)
  7697	    launch.bind_process(process)
  7698	    # The launch is registered with the table of the launcher: the
  7699	    # recovery of the table coordinates the resources of the launch in
  7700	    # the dependency order - the channel before the process.
  7701	    table.retain('aggregate', launch, 'launch')
  7702	    table.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=launch)
  7703	    channel = None
  7704	    try:
  7705	        channel = channel_factory()
  7706	        if channel is not None:
  7707	            launch.bind_channel(channel)
  7708	        broker = BrokerProcess(process, executable, executable_sha256, channel,
  7709	                               context=launch.context)
  7710	    except BaseException as error:
  7711	        # The resources of the launch are coordinated by the aggregate:
  7712	        # the channel first and the process after it.  The resources
  7713	        # that could not be released keep their original reference and
  7714	        # their owner.
  7715	        #
  7716	        # A **refusal of the admission** of the launch is not a failure
  7717	        # of the resources of the launch: the process that was already
  7718	        # admitted keeps its owner in the table of the launcher and it
  7719	        # stays reachable and recoverable by the recovery of the
  7720	        # launcher.  The launch never pretends to undo the operating
  7721	        # system work that it really admitted.
  7722	        if not _is_startup_refusal(error):
  7723	            try:
  7724	                launch.release(deadline=deadline)
  7725	            except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit) as cancellation:
  7726	                # The cancellation of the release never replaces the
  7727	                # original failure of the launch: the original object
  7728	                # continues to propagate and the cancellation stays
  7729	                # **reachable** next to it.  The primary failure and the
  7730	                # cancellation of the recovery coexist.
  7731	                try:
  7732	                    error.lvw_cancelled = cancellation
  7733	                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7734	                    pass
  7735	            except BaseException:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7736	                pass
  7737	        raise
  7738	    # The handover happened: the launcher does not own the process of
  7739	    # the round any more.
  7740	    table.forget(process)
  7741	    table.forget(channel)
  7742	    table.forget(launch)
  7743	    launch.forget(channel)
  7744	    launch.forget(process)
  7745	    return broker
  7746	
  7747	
  7748	def _check_launch_budget(context, stage):
  7749	    """Check the budget of the round before a side effect of a launch.
  7750	
  7751	    The check is asked **before** the first side effect of the launch
  7752	    - the creation of the directory, the writing of the plan and the
  7753	    creation of the process - and it is asked **again** after the
  7754	    preparatory work and before the next effect.  A launch that starts
  7755	    after the deadline of the round arrived never creates a process.
  7756	
  7757	    The **original** context of the round is the authority: the
  7758	    launcher never keeps a copy of the deadline and it never opens a
  7759	    budget of its own.  The authority is the **effective minimum** of
  7760	    the context - the absolute deadline of the round **and** the
  7761	    published cleanup deadline of the round - and not the absolute
  7762	    deadline alone: a round that published its cleanup deadline and
  7763	    whose cleanup expired is a round that may not create a process any
  7764	    more, even when its absolute lease is still in the future.  The
  7765	    stop of the round, the cancel of the round and the unknown state of
  7766	    the round are honoured here as well.
  7767	    """
  7768	    if context is None:
  7769	        return True
  7770	    if context.cancel.is_set() or context.is_unknown():
  7771	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
  7772	    if context.stopping.is_set():
  7773	        # The public stop of the round published the stop of the round:
  7774	        # a launch that continues after it would create a process that
  7775	        # nobody may stop any more.
  7776	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_CANCELLED', stage)
  7777	    deadline = context.effective_deadline()
  7778	    if deadline is not None and context.clock() >= deadline:
  7779	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', stage)
  7780	    return True
  7781	
  7782	
  7783	def launch_broker(plan, run_root, nonce, *, context=None):
  7784	    """Start the broker process of one round and keep its original handle.
  7785	
  7786	    The launcher is a Windows only operation.  It refuses on every other
  7787	    platform before anything is started: a broker that cannot be started
  7788	    on Windows is never replaced by an unconstrained process and never by
  7789	    a simulation that pretends to be one.
  7790	
  7791	    The nonce and the plan are checked **before** the first directory and
  7792	    the first file of the round exist, so that a refused launch leaves
  7793	    nothing behind.
  7794	
  7795	    The ONE context of the round is held **before** the first side
  7796	    effect of the launch: the creation of the process, the reading of
  7797	    the executable, the channel and its endpoints all read the deadline
  7798	    of the round from that context.  A launcher that waits for the end
  7799	    of the launcher to bind the deadline would leave a window in which
  7800	    the process of the round runs without a budget.
  7801	
  7802	    The **aggregate** of the launch is created before the process and it
  7803	    holds every original resource of the launch: a failure at any point
  7804	    of the launch finds the partial owner of the launch through it.
  7805	    """
  7806	    if os.name != 'nt':
  7807	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLATFORM_UNSUPPORTED')
  7808	    if plan is None:
  7809	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID')
  7810	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(nonce)):
  7811	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_NONCE_INVALID')
  7812	    # The aggregate of this launch and the deadline of the round are
  7813	    # taken **before** the first side effect of the launch.
  7814	    launch = _LaunchAggregate(custody=_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY, context=context)
  7815	    launch.bind_context(context)
  7816	    # The table of the launcher reads the deadline of the round from
  7817	    # the same context: a recovery of the launcher never opens a fresh
  7818	    # budget of its own.
  7819	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.bind_context(context)
  7820	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.retain('aggregate', launch, 'launch')
  7821	    deadline = None
  7822	    if context is not None:
  7823	        deadline = context.absolute_deadline
  7824	    root = Path(run_root)
  7825	    raw = plan.sealed_bytes()
  7826	    digest = plan.sealed_sha256()
  7827	    directory = root / 'temp' / 'native'
  7828	    # The budget of the round is checked **before** the first side
  7829	    # effect of the launch: a launch that starts after the deadline of
  7830	    # the round arrived creates no directory and no file.
  7831	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_DIRECTORY')
  7832	    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  7833	    plan_path = directory / 'custody-plan.json'
  7834	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_PLAN')
  7835	    with plan_path.open('xb') as stream:
  7836	        stream.write(raw)
  7837	    command = build_broker_command(plan_path=plan_path, plan_sha256=digest,
  7838	                                   run_root=root, nonce=nonce)
  7839	    # The budget is checked **again** after the preparatory work and
  7840	    # before the creation of the process: a plan file that consumed the
  7841	    # whole lease of the round never produces a process.
  7842	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_POPEN')
  7843	    process = subprocess.Popen(command, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
  7844	                               stderr=None, close_fds=True)
  7845	    # The original process of the round is with the launcher from the
  7846	    # moment of its creation.  The window between the creation of the
  7847	    # process and the handover of the channel is not a window without an
  7848	    # owner: a cancellation of the reading of the executable leaves the
  7849	    # process with its owner and it never disappears.
  7850	    launch.bind_process(process)
  7851	    _LAUNCHER_CUSTODY.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=launch)
  7852	    executable = Path(command[0])
  7853	    # The **next** distinct effect of the launch is the read of the
  7854	    # executable.  The public stop of the round, the cancel of the
  7855	    # round, the unknown state of the round and the **current**
  7856	    # effective minimum of the round are asked again **before** it: a
  7857	    # stop that was published while the process was admitted refuses the
  7858	    # hash.  The process of the round is **not** undone - it keeps its
  7859	    # owner in the table of the launcher and it stays reachable and
  7860	    # recoverable by the recovery of the launcher.
  7861	    _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_HASH')
  7862	    try:
  7863	        executable_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
  7864	    except OSError:
  7865	        executable_sha256 = None
  7866	    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
  7867	        # The cancellation of the reading of the executable is cleaned
  7868	        # up and it continues to propagate.  The process keeps its owner
  7869	        # in the table of the launcher and the recovery of the launcher
  7870	        # is the entry that releases it.
  7871	        raise
  7872	
  7873	    def channel_factory():
  7874	        """Build the channel of this launch with the context of the round.
  7875	
  7876	        The channel registers itself with the **aggregate** of the
  7877	        launch before it builds anything: a channel whose reader
  7878	        thread did not start is found by the outer owner through the
  7879	        aggregate and the files of the process are never closed by the
  7880	        process helper.
  7881	
  7882	        The admission of the round is asked **again** immediately
  7883	        before the channel is created: a stop that was published while
  7884	        the hash of the executable was paused refuses the channel and
  7885	        the process of the round keeps its owner.
  7886	        """
  7887	        _check_launch_budget(context, 'LAUNCH:BEFORE_CHANNEL')
  7888	        channel = StreamChannel(process.stdout, process.stdin,
  7889	                                custody=launch, context=context)
  7890	        if deadline is not None:
  7891	            channel.bind_deadline(deadline)
  7892	        return channel
  7893	
  7894	    return _adopt_broker_process(
  7895	        process,
  7896	        channel_factory=channel_factory,
  7897	        executable=str(executable), executable_sha256=executable_sha256,
  7898	        deadline=deadline, aggregate=launch)
  7899	
  7900	
  7901	def read_sealed_plan(path, digest):
  7902	    """Read one sealed plan document and check its digest."""
  7903	    target = Path(path)
  7904	    if not target.is_file():
  7905	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_MISSING')
  7906	    with target.open('rb') as stream:
  7907	        raw = stream.read(MAX_PLAN_BYTES + 1)
  7908	    if len(raw) > MAX_PLAN_BYTES:
  7909	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_TOO_LARGE')
  7910	    if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != digest:
  7911	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_DIGEST_DRIFT')
  7912	    try:
  7913	        document = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'))
  7914	    except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as error:
  7915	        raise BrokerError('BROKER_PLAN_INVALID') from error
  7916	    return document
  7917	
  7918	
  7919	def main(argv=None):
  7920	    """The production entry of the broker.
  7921	
  7922	    The entry is fixed: one sealed plan, its digest, the run root of the
  7923	    round and the nonce of the session.  The plan is validated again here,
  7924	    because the broker never trusts a document that it did not check.
  7925	    """
  7926	    import argparse
  7927	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
  7928	    parser.add_argument('--plan', required=True)
  7929	    parser.add_argument('--plan-sha256', required=True)
  7930	    parser.add_argument('--run-root', required=True)
  7931	    parser.add_argument('--nonce', required=True)
  7932	    arguments = parser.parse_args(argv)
  7933	    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}', str(arguments.nonce)):
  7934	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_NONCE_INVALID\n')
  7935	        sys.stderr.flush()
  7936	        return 2
  7937	    document = read_sealed_plan(arguments.plan, arguments.plan_sha256)
  7938	    try:
  7939	        plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
  7940	    except CustodyError as error:
  7941	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
  7942	        sys.stderr.flush()
  7943	        return 2
  7944	    if plan.sealed_sha256() != arguments.plan_sha256:
  7945	        # The seal of the plan that the broker validated is not the seal
  7946	        # that the parent wrote: the document drifted on its way here.
  7947	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED BROKER_PLAN_SEAL_DRIFT\n')
  7948	        sys.stderr.flush()
  7949	        return 2
  7950	    try:
  7951	        win32 = WindowsApi()
  7952	    except BrokerError as error:
  7953	        sys.stderr.write('BROKER_REFUSED ' + error.code + '\n')
  7954	        sys.stderr.flush()
  7955	        return 2
  7956	    core = BrokerCore(win32=win32, plan=plan, run_root=arguments.run_root)
  7957	    loop = BrokerLoop(core=core, transport=None, plan=plan,
  7958	                      nonce=arguments.nonce)
  7959	    # The **absolute deadline** of the round is fixed and bound to the
  7960	    # core **before** the transport of the round exists.  The reader of
  7961	    # the channel and the workers of the endpoints therefore never
  7962	    # start with an unbound context: the child has no window in which
  7963	    # a read of the channel runs without a deadline.
  7964	    deadline = loop.fix_deadline()
  7965	    core.bind_absolute_deadline(deadline)
  7966	    # The ONE context of the round is handed to the transport of the
  7967	    # channel and to its endpoints: the channel, the endpoints and the
  7968	    # workers of the endpoints read the same absolute deadline and none
  7969	    # of them keeps a budget of its own.
  7970	    transport = StreamChannel(sys.stdin.buffer, sys.stdout.buffer, api=win32,
  7971	                              context=core.context)
  7972	    transport.bind_deadline(deadline)
  7973	    loop.bind_transport(transport)
  7974	    try:
  7975	        return loop.run()
  7976	    finally:
  7977	        # The round releases its own resources in the loop; the entry only
  7978	        # closes the channel and never swallows a failure of the round.
  7979	        try:
  7980	            transport.close()
  7981	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
  7982	            pass
  7983	
  7984	
  7985	if __name__ == '__main__':
  7986	    sys.exit(main())
